
===== FILE backend/app/mysql_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import plistlib
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import signal
import socket as socket_module
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import CompletedProcess
from typing import Any, Literal, TextIO

from sqlalchemy import URL
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from .database_capabilities import DatabaseDialectError, parse_mysql_version
from .security import protect_secret, unprotect_secret


INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT = Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD")
MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT = (
    INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / "3d66-label-system-integration" / "mysql84"
)
PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES = 120 * 1024**3
MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES = 40 * 1024**3
MYSQL_PORT = 33084
_INSTANCE_FORMAT = "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1"
_ACCOUNTS_FORMAT = "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1"


class IntegrationPathError(ValueError):
    """Raised when local MySQL files could escape the approved SSD root."""


class IntegrationCapacityError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the local integration workspace breaches its disk gate."""


class MySQLIntegrationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the isolated MySQL 8.4 instance cannot be managed safely."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLBinaries:
    mysqld: Path
    mysql: Path
    mysqladmin: Path

    @classmethod
    def for_prefix(cls, prefix: Path) -> "MySQLBinaries":
        binary_dir = prefix.expanduser().resolve(strict=False) / "bin"
        return cls(
            mysqld=binary_dir / "mysqld",
            mysql=binary_dir / "mysql",
            mysqladmin=binary_dir / "mysqladmin",
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLIntegrationPaths:
    root: Path
    datadir: Path
    socket_dir: Path
    socket: Path
    logs: Path
    checkpoints: Path
    reports: Path
    config: Path
    pid_file: Path
    accounts_file: Path
    instance_marker: Path
    port: int = MYSQL_PORT

    @classmethod
    def from_root(cls, root: Path) -> "MySQLIntegrationPaths":
        absolute_root = root.expanduser().absolute()
        return cls(
            root=absolute_root,
            datadir=absolute_root / "data",
            socket_dir=absolute_root / "socket",
            socket=absolute_root / "socket" / "mysql.sock",
            logs=absolute_root / "logs",
            checkpoints=absolute_root / "checkpoints",
            reports=absolute_root / "reports",
            config=absolute_root / "my.cnf",
            pid_file=absolute_root / "mysql.pid",
            accounts_file=absolute_root / "accounts.json",
            instance_marker=absolute_root / "instance.json",
        )

    def assert_safe(self) -> None:
        approved_volume = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.expanduser().absolute()
        approved_root = MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT.expanduser().absolute()
        if self.root != approved_root or self.root.parent.parent != approved_volume:
            raise IntegrationPathError(
                "MySQL 8.4 集成数据只能位于 WorkSSD 的项目专属 mysql84 目录"
            )
        if self.root.is_symlink():
            raise IntegrationPathError("MySQL 8.4 项目目录不得使用符号链接")
        for child in (
            self.datadir,
            self.socket_dir,
            self.logs,
            self.checkpoints,
            self.reports,
            self.config,
            self.pid_file,
            self.accounts_file,
            self.instance_marker,
        ):
            if child.is_symlink():
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录内不得使用符号链接：{child.name}"
                )
            if child.exists() and not child.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(
                self.root.resolve(strict=True)
            ):
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录子路径越界：{child.name}"
                )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLAccountReferences:
    root_user: str
    root_password_reference: str
    migration_user: str
    migration_password_reference: str
    app_user: str
    app_password_reference: str
    test_user: str
    test_password_reference: str


CommandRunner = Callable[[Sequence[str]], CompletedProcess[str]]
ProcessStarter = Callable[..., Any]


def _directory_size(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0
    total = 0
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        directory = pending.pop()
        with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
            for entry in entries:
                if entry.is_symlink():
                    raise IntegrationPathError(
                        f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录内不得使用符号链接：{entry.name}"
                    )
                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                    pending.append(Path(entry.path))
                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                    total += entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False).st_size
    return total


def integration_disk_report(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
) -> dict[str, int | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    project_used_bytes = _directory_size(paths.root)
    probe = paths.root if paths.root.exists() else paths.root.parent
    disk = shutil.disk_usage(probe)
    if project_used_bytes >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "MySQL 8.4 项目目录已达到 120 GiB 上限，禁止继续写入"
        )
    if disk.free < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "MySQL 8.4 数据盘可用空间低于 40 GiB，禁止继续写入"
        )
    return {
        "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
        "project_used_bytes": project_used_bytes,
        "project_limit_bytes": PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": disk.total,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": disk.free,
        "minimum_free_bytes": MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES,
    }


def _default_run_command(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        tuple(command),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )


def _run_checked(
    command: Sequence[str],
    run_command: CommandRunner,
) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    completed = run_command(tuple(command))
    if int(getattr(completed, "returncode", 1)) != 0:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"MySQL 8.4 命令执行失败（exit {completed.returncode}）"
        )
    return completed


def _require_binaries(binaries: MySQLBinaries) -> None:
    missing = [
        str(path)
        for path in (binaries.mysqld, binaries.mysql, binaries.mysqladmin)
        if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path, os.X_OK)
    ]
    if missing:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            "MySQL 8.4 可执行文件不完整：" + "、".join(missing)
        )


def _port_available(port: int) -> bool:
    probe = socket_module.socket(socket_module.AF_INET, socket_module.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        probe.setsockopt(socket_module.SOL_SOCKET, socket_module.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        probe.bind(("127.0.0.1", port))
    except OSError:
        return False
    finally:
        probe.close()
    return True


def _prepare_directories(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if not INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.is_dir():
        raise IntegrationPathError("WorkSSD 未挂载，禁止创建 MySQL 8.4 集成目录")
    paths.root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(paths.root, 0o700)
    for directory in (
        paths.datadir,
        paths.socket_dir,
        paths.logs,
        paths.checkpoints,
        paths.reports,
    ):
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(directory, 0o700)
    paths.assert_safe()


def _atomic_write_text(path: Path, content: str, *, mode: int = 0o600) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
        text=True,
    )
    temporary = Path(temporary_name)
    try:
        os.fchmod(descriptor, mode)
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
        os.chmod(path, mode)
    finally:
        if temporary.exists():
            temporary.unlink()


def _mysql_configuration(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        (
            "# Managed by 3d66 label-system local integration tooling.",
            "[mysqld]",
            f"datadir={paths.datadir}",
            f"socket={paths.socket}",
            f"pid-file={paths.pid_file}",
            f"log-error={paths.logs / 'mysql-error.log'}",
            "bind-address=127.0.0.1",
            f"port={paths.port}",
            "mysqlx=0",
            "skip-name-resolve=ON",
            "default-storage-engine=InnoDB",
            "transaction-isolation=READ-COMMITTED",
            "character-set-server=utf8mb4",
            "collation-server=utf8mb4_0900_as_cs",
            "default-time-zone=+00:00",
            "local-infile=0",
            "symbolic-links=0",
            "secure-file-priv=NULL",
            "innodb-flush-log-at-trx-commit=1",
            "log-bin-trust-function-creators=ON",
            "binlog-expire-logs-auto-purge=ON",
            "binlog-expire-logs-seconds=86400",
            "sql-mode=STRICT_TRANS_TABLES,ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO,NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION",
            "",
            "[client]",
            f"socket={paths.socket}",
            f"port={paths.port}",
            "default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            "",
        )
    )


def _read_json_object(path: Path, *, description: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}不存在或不是普通文件")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}无法读取") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}必须是 JSON 对象")
    return payload


def _verified_instance_marker(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not paths.instance_marker.exists():
        return None
    marker = _read_json_object(paths.instance_marker, description="MySQL 8.4 实例标记")
    if (
        marker.get("format") != _INSTANCE_FORMAT
        or marker.get("root") != str(paths.root.resolve())
        or marker.get("port") != paths.port
        or not paths.datadir.is_dir()
        or paths.datadir.is_symlink()
    ):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 实例标记与当前目录不一致")
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(str(marker.get("version", "")))
    except DatabaseDialectError as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("已有实例不是 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x") from exc
    return marker


def _binary_version(
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    run_command: CommandRunner,
) -> str:
    completed = _run_checked((str(binaries.mysqld), "--version"), run_command)
    output = f"{completed.stdout}\n{completed.stderr}".strip()
    match = re.search(r"\b(\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:[-+][^\s]+)?)", output)
    if match is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法识别 mysqld 版本；本地实例必须使用 MySQL 8.4")
    version = match.group(1)
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(version)
    except DatabaseDialectError as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x；实际为 {version}"
        ) from exc
    if "mariadb" in output.lower():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("本地集成必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x")
    return version


def _default_filesystem_type(path: Path) -> str:
    completed = _default_run_command(
        ("/usr/sbin/diskutil", "info", "-plist", str(path))
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法识别 WorkSSD 文件系统")
    try:
        payload = plistlib.loads(completed.stdout.encode("utf-8"))
    except (plistlib.InvalidFileException, ValueError) as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法解析 WorkSSD 文件系统信息") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息不是属性字典")
    filesystem = payload.get("FilesystemType") or payload.get("FilesystemName")
    if not isinstance(filesystem, str) or not filesystem.strip():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息缺少类型")
    return filesystem.strip().lower()


def preflight_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    require_executables: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, int | str | bool]:
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    volume = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=True)
    filesystem = filesystem_type(volume).strip().lower()
    if filesystem != "apfs":
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"MySQL 8.4 集成数据盘必须使用 APFS，当前识别为 {filesystem or '未知'}"
        )
    if require_executables:
        _require_binaries(binaries)
    version = _binary_version(binaries, run_command)
    return {
        **report,
        "filesystem": filesystem,
        "mysql_version": version,
        "local_only": True,
        "port": paths.port,
        "port_available": _port_available(paths.port),
    }


def initialize_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if _verified_instance_marker(paths) is not None:
        return
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _require_binaries(binaries)
    version = _binary_version(binaries, run_command)
    if not _port_available(paths.port):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成端口 {paths.port} 已被占用，禁止初始化 MySQL 8.4"
        )
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    if any(paths.datadir.iterdir()):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 data 目录非空但缺少实例标记，禁止覆盖")
    _atomic_write_text(paths.config, _mysql_configuration(paths))
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--initialize-insecure",
        ),
        run_command,
    )
    auto_config = paths.datadir / "auto.cnf"
    if auto_config.is_symlink() or not auto_config.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("mysqld 初始化后未生成 data/auto.cnf")
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.instance_marker,
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": _INSTANCE_FORMAT,
                "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
                "port": paths.port,
                "version": version,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            indent=2,
        )
        + "\n",
    )


def _wait_for_mysql_ready(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    timeout_seconds: float = 30.0,
) -> None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_seconds
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if paths.pid_file.is_file() and (
            paths.socket.is_socket() or not _port_available(paths.port)
        ):
            return
        time.sleep(0.1)
    raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 未在 30 秒内就绪")


def start_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    start_process: ProcessStarter = subprocess.Popen,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    _require_binaries(binaries)
    if paths.config.is_symlink() or not paths.config.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 配置文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    if paths.pid_file.exists():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件已存在，请先检查实例状态")
    start_process(
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--daemonize",
        ),
        stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        close_fds=True,
    )
    _wait_for_mysql_ready(paths)


def _default_process_command(pid: int) -> str:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        ("/bin/ps", "-p", str(pid), "-o", "command="),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return completed.stdout.strip() if completed.returncode == 0 else ""


def _default_terminate_pid(pid: int) -> None:
    os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)


def _read_pid(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> int | None:
    if not paths.pid_file.exists():
        return None
    if paths.pid_file.is_symlink() or not paths.pid_file.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    raw_pid = paths.pid_file.read_text(encoding="ascii").strip()
    if re.fullmatch(r"[1-9][0-9]{1,9}", raw_pid) is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件内容不合法")
    return int(raw_pid)


def _is_project_mysql_process(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    pid: int,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str],
) -> bool:
    command = process_command(pid)
    return "mysqld" in command and f"--defaults-file={paths.config}" in command


def mysql_status(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str] = _default_process_command,
) -> dict[str, bool | int | str | None]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    pid = _read_pid(paths)
    running = pid is not None and _is_project_mysql_process(
        paths, pid, process_command
    )
    return {
        "running": running,
        "pid": pid if running else None,
        "root": str(paths.root),
        "socket": str(paths.socket),
        "port": paths.port,
    }


def stop_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str] = _default_process_command,
    terminate_pid: Callable[[int], None] = _default_terminate_pid,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    pid = _read_pid(paths)
    if pid is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    if not _is_project_mysql_process(paths, pid, process_command):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("PID 对应进程不属于本项目 MySQL 8.4 实例，拒绝停止")
    terminate_pid(pid)


def bootstrap_mysql_accounts(
    connection: Connection,
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    protect_secret_fn: Callable[..., str] = protect_secret,
    password_factory: Callable[[], str] = lambda: secrets.token_urlsafe(48),
) -> MySQLAccountReferences:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户只能在 MySQL 连接上初始化")
    account_specs = (
        ("root", "mysql84-root"),
        ("labelsystem_migration", "mysql84-migration"),
        ("labelsystem_app", "mysql84-app"),
        ("labelsystem_test_admin", "mysql84-test-admin"),
    )
    passwords = {user: password_factory() for user, _account in account_specs}
    references = {
        user: protect_secret_fn(passwords[user], account=account)
        for user, account in account_specs
    }
    statements = (
        ("ALTER USER 'root'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s", "root"),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_migration'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_migration",
        ),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_app'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_app",
        ),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_test_admin'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_test_admin",
        ),
    )
    for statement, user in statements:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(statement, (passwords[user],))
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `labelsystem\\_integration\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_migration'@'localhost'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON `labelsystem\\_integration\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_app'@'localhost'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `labelsystem\\_test\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_test_admin'@'localhost'"
    )
    result = MySQLAccountReferences(
        root_user="root",
        root_password_reference=references["root"],
        migration_user="labelsystem_migration",
        migration_password_reference=references["labelsystem_migration"],
        app_user="labelsystem_app",
        app_password_reference=references["labelsystem_app"],
        test_user="labelsystem_test_admin",
        test_password_reference=references["labelsystem_test_admin"],
    )
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.accounts_file,
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": _ACCOUNTS_FORMAT,
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": paths.port,
                "root_user": result.root_user,
                "root_password_reference": result.root_password_reference,
                "migration_user": result.migration_user,
                "migration_password_reference": result.migration_password_reference,
                "app_user": result.app_user,
                "app_password_reference": result.app_password_reference,
                "test_user": result.test_user,
                "test_password_reference": result.test_password_reference,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            indent=2,
        )
        + "\n",
    )
    return result


def _validate_accounts_payload(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for key in payload:
        normalized = str(key).lower()
        if "password" in normalized and not normalized.endswith(
            "_password_reference"
        ):
            raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件不得包含明文密码字段")
    if payload.get("format") != _ACCOUNTS_FORMAT:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件格式不受支持")


def mysql_database_url_from_references(
    accounts_file: Path,
    *,
    role: Literal["root", "migration", "app", "test"],
    database: str | None,
) -> URL:
    resolved_file = accounts_file.expanduser().absolute()
    if resolved_file.is_symlink() or not resolved_file.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置不存在或不是普通文件")
    paths = MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(resolved_file.parent)
    paths.assert_safe()
    if resolved_file != paths.accounts_file:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件路径不受支持")
    if resolved_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 != 0o600:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置权限必须为 0600")
    payload = _read_json_object(resolved_file, description="MySQL 账户配置")
    _validate_accounts_payload(payload)
    if role not in {"root", "migration", "app", "test"}:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户角色不受支持")
    expected_users = {
        "root": "root",
        "migration": "labelsystem_migration",
        "app": "labelsystem_app",
        "test": "labelsystem_test_admin",
    }
    username = payload.get(f"{role}_user")
    reference = payload.get(f"{role}_password_reference")
    if username != expected_users[role] or not isinstance(reference, str):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置中的角色引用不完整")
    if payload.get("socket") != str(paths.socket) or payload.get("port") != paths.port:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置与本地实例地址不一致")
    if database is not None and re.fullmatch(
        r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", database
    ) is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 数据库名不合法")
    password = unprotect_secret(reference)
    return URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username=username,
        password=password,
        host="localhost",
        port=paths.port,
        database=database,
        query={"unix_socket": str(paths.socket), "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )


def _cli_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Manage the isolated 3d66 label-system MySQL 8.4 instance."
    )
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for command_name in ("preflight", "init", "start", "stop", "status"):
        command = commands.add_parser(command_name)
        command.add_argument(
            "--root",
            type=Path,
            default=MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT,
        )
        command.add_argument(
            "--mysql-prefix",
            type=Path,
            default=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/mysql@8.4"),
        )
    return parser


def cli_main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    start_process: ProcessStarter = subprocess.Popen,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    args = _cli_parser().parse_args(argv)
    paths = MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(args.root)
    binaries = MySQLBinaries.for_prefix(args.mysql_prefix)
    if args.command == "preflight":
        payload: dict[str, object] = preflight_mysql(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
    elif args.command == "init":
        preflight_mysql(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        initialize_mysql(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = {"initialized": True}
    elif args.command == "start":
        start_mysql(paths, binaries, start_process=start_process)
        payload = mysql_status(paths)
    elif args.command == "stop":
        stop_mysql(paths)
        payload = {"stopped": True}
    else:
        payload = mysql_status(paths)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "ok",
                "command": args.command,
                "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
                **payload,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        ),
        file=output,
    )
    return 0

_BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES = frozenset(
    {
        "trg_mysql_dimension_schemas_no_self_insert",
        "trg_mysql_dimension_schemas_no_self_update",
    }
)
_EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS = frozenset(
    contract.name
    for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
)
_EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS = frozenset(
    str(constraint.name)
    for table in mysql_metadata().tables.values()
    for constraint in table.constraints
    if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
)
_MIN_FORWARD_COMPATIBLE_SOURCE_VERSION = 81


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AutoIncrementState:
    table_name: str
    primary_key_max: int | None
    next_value: int | None

    @property
    def safe(self) -> bool:
        minimum = 1 if self.primary_key_max is None else self.primary_key_max + 1
        return self.next_value is not None and self.next_value >= minimum


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLReconciliationReport:
    source: Path
    source_sha256: str
    source_quick_check: str
    source_schema_version: int | None
    target_schema: str
    target_schema_version: int | None
    target_mysql_version: str
    source_foreign_key_violations: int
    target_foreign_key_violations: dict[str, int]
    schema_facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation]
    auto_increments: dict[str, AutoIncrementState]
    generated_unique_guards: tuple[str, ...]
    generated_guard_indexes: tuple[str, ...]
    source_uri_hash_contract: bool
    installed_invariant_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_baseline_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_check_constraints: tuple[str, ...]
    forward_compatible_source: bool

    @property
    def data_evidence_sha256(self) -> str:
        """Canonical content evidence independent of the target schema name."""

        payload = {
            "source_sha256": self.source_sha256,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "tables": {
                name: asdict(table)
                for name, table in sorted(self.tables.items())
            },
            "source_foreign_key_violations": self.source_foreign_key_violations,
            "target_foreign_key_violations": dict(
                sorted(self.target_foreign_key_violations.items())
            ),
        }
        encoded = json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "source": str(self.source),
            "source_sha256": self.source_sha256,
            "source_quick_check": self.source_quick_check,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema": self.target_schema,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "target_mysql_version": self.target_mysql_version,
            "source_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.source_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "target_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.target_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "schema_facts": self.schema_facts,
            "tables": {
                name: {**asdict(table), "matches": table.matches}
                for name, table in self.tables.items()
            },
            "auto_increments": {
                name: {**asdict(state), "safe": state.safe}
                for name, state in self.auto_increments.items()
            },
            "generated_unique_guards": list(self.generated_unique_guards),
            "generated_guard_indexes": list(self.generated_guard_indexes),
            "source_uri_hash_contract": self.source_uri_hash_contract,
            "installed_invariant_triggers": list(
                self.installed_invariant_triggers
            ),
            "installed_baseline_triggers": list(
                self.installed_baseline_triggers
            ),
            "installed_triggers": list(self.installed_triggers),
            "installed_check_constraints": list(
                self.installed_check_constraints
            ),
            "forward_compatible_source": self.forward_compatible_source,
            "data_evidence_sha256": self.data_evidence_sha256,
        }

    def to_markdown(self, *, accepted: bool, error: str = "") -> str:
        mismatches = [
            name for name, table in self.tables.items() if not table.matches
        ]
        target_fk_total = sum(self.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
        lines = [
            "# SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告",
            "",
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}",
            f"- 源快照：`{self.source}`",
            f"- 源 SHA-256：`{self.source_sha256}`",
            f"- 目标 schema：`{self.target_schema}`",
            f"- MySQL：`{self.target_mysql_version}`",
            f"- 数据证据 SHA-256：`{self.data_evidence_sha256}`",
            f"- 表：{len(self.tables)}",
            "- 源/目标业务行："
            f"{sum(item.source_row_count for item in self.tables.values())} / "
            f"{sum(item.target_row_count for item in self.tables.values())}",
            f"- 行级不一致表：{len(mismatches)}",
            f"- SQLite 外键违规：{self.source_foreign_key_violations}",
            f"- MySQL 外键违规：{target_fk_total}",
            f"- MySQL 不变量触发器：{len(self.installed_invariant_triggers)}",
            f"- MySQL 生成列唯一约束：{len(self.generated_unique_guards)}",
            f"- Schema 版本：{self.source_schema_version} → "
            f"{self.target_schema_version}",
            "- 向上兼容旧快照："
            f"{'是' if self.forward_compatible_source else '否'}",
        ]
        if mismatches:
            lines.extend(("", "## 不一致表", ""))
            lines.extend(f"- `{name}`" for name in mismatches)
        if error:
            lines.extend(("", "## 阻断原因", "", error))
        lines.append("")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _source_rows(
    connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    table_name: str,
    column_names: tuple[str, ...],
    primary_key_name: str,
    *,
    source_columns: set[str],
    synthetic_defaults: dict[str, Any],
) -> Any:
    selected_names = [name for name in column_names if name in source_columns]
    if primary_key_name not in selected_names:
        raise ReconciliationError(
            f"SQLite 表 {table_name} 缺少主键列 {primary_key_name}"
        )
    selected = ", ".join(
        sqlite_identifier(name) for name in selected_names
    )
    cursor = connection.execute(
        f"SELECT {selected} FROM {sqlite_identifier(table_name)} "
        f"ORDER BY {sqlite_identifier(primary_key_name)}"
    )
    while rows := cursor.fetchmany(500):
        for row in rows:
            normalized = {name: row[name] for name in selected_names}
            normalized.update(synthetic_defaults)
            yield normalized


def _source_schema_version(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int | None:
    exists = connection.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master "
        "WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_migrations'"
    ).fetchone()
    if exists is None:
        return None
    value = connection.execute(
        "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
    ).fetchone()[0]
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def _expected_generated_columns(table_name: str) -> set[str]:
    generated = {
        spec.guard_column
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
        if spec.table_name == table_name
    }
    if table_name == "assets":
        generated.add("source_uri_sha256")
    return generated


def _schema_facts(
    source: sqlite3.Connection,
    target: Connection,
    *,
    source_schema_version: int | None,
    source_columns_by_table: dict[str, set[str]],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    target_inspector = inspect(target)
    facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        source_rows = source.execute(
            f"PRAGMA table_info({sqlite_identifier(table.name)})"
        ).fetchall()
        source_columns = [str(row[1]) for row in source_rows]
        source_primary_key = [
            str(row[1])
            for row in sorted(source_rows, key=lambda row: row[5])
            if row[5]
        ]
        target_columns_info = target_inspector.get_columns(table.name)
        all_target_columns = [
            str(column["name"]) for column in target_columns_info
        ]
        expected_generated = _expected_generated_columns(table.name)
        target_columns = [
            name for name in all_target_columns if name not in expected_generated
        ]
        target_primary_key = list(
            target_inspector.get_pk_constraint(table.name).get(
                "constrained_columns"
            )
            or []
        )
        generated_facts = {
            str(column["name"]): str(column.get("computed") or "")
            for column in target_columns_info
            if str(column["name"]) in expected_generated
        }
        source_absent = table.name not in source_columns_by_table
        synthetic_defaults = {
            name: value
            for name, value in forward_compatible_column_defaults(
                source_schema_version,
                table.name,
            ).items()
            if name not in source_columns
        }
        effective_source_columns = source_columns + list(
            synthetic_defaults
        )
        compatible = (
            source_absent
            and table.name
            in forward_compatible_empty_tables(source_schema_version)
        ) or schema_contract_compatible(
            source_columns=effective_source_columns,
            target_columns=target_columns,
            source_primary_key=source_primary_key,
            target_primary_key=target_primary_key,
        )
        facts[table.name] = {
            "source_columns": source_columns,
            "effective_source_columns": effective_source_columns,
            "target_columns": target_columns,
            "target_all_columns": all_target_columns,
            "target_generated_columns": generated_facts,
            "source_primary_key": source_primary_key,
            "target_primary_key": target_primary_key,
            "source_absent": source_absent,
            "synthetic_default_columns": sorted(synthetic_defaults),
            "source_declared_types": {
                str(row[1]): str(row[2]) for row in source_rows
            },
            "target_declared_types": {
                str(column["name"]): str(column["type"])
                for column in target_columns_info
                if str(column["name"]) not in expected_generated
            },
            "compatible": compatible,
        }
    return facts


def _target_foreign_key_violations(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    violations: dict[str, int] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for ordinal, constraint in enumerate(
            sorted(
                table.foreign_key_constraints,
                key=lambda item: item.name or "",
            )
        ):
            elements = tuple(constraint.elements)
            child = table.alias(f"fk_child_{table.name}_{ordinal}")
            parent_table = elements[0].column.table
            parent = parent_table.alias(f"fk_parent_{table.name}_{ordinal}")
            join_condition = and_(
                *(
                    child.c[element.parent.name]
                    == parent.c[element.column.name]
                    for element in elements
                )
            )
            local_values_present = and_(
                *(child.c[element.parent.name].is_not(None) for element in elements)
            )
            missing_parent = parent.c[elements[0].column.name].is_(None)
            key = (
                f"{table.name}."
                + ",".join(element.parent.name for element in elements)
                + "->"
                + f"{parent_table.name}."
                + ",".join(element.column.name for element in elements)
            )
            count = connection.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(child.outerjoin(parent, join_condition))
                .where(local_values_present, missing_parent)
            )
            violations[key] = int(count or 0)
    return violations


def _auto_increment_states(
    connection: Connection,
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation],
) -> dict[str, AutoIncrementState]:
    # MySQL 8.4 caches TABLES.AUTO_INCREMENT statistics for up to 24 hours.
    # Reconciliation needs the storage-engine value from this committed import.
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SET SESSION information_schema_stats_expiry = 0"
    )
    next_values = {
        str(row.TABLE_NAME): (
            None if row.AUTO_INCREMENT is None else int(row.AUTO_INCREMENT)
        )
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TABLE_NAME, AUTO_INCREMENT "
                "FROM information_schema.TABLES "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND TABLE_TYPE='BASE TABLE'"
            )
        )
    }
    states: dict[str, AutoIncrementState] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
        if (
            not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer)
            or table._autoincrement_column is None
        ):
            continue
        maximum = tables[table.name].target_primary_key_max
        states[table.name] = AutoIncrementState(
            table_name=table.name,
            primary_key_max=None if maximum is None else int(maximum),
            next_value=next_values.get(table.name),
        )
    return states


def _index_inventory(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[bool, tuple[str, ...]]]:
    rows = connection.execute(
        text(
            "SELECT TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME, NON_UNIQUE, SEQ_IN_INDEX, "
            "COLUMN_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() "
            "ORDER BY TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME, SEQ_IN_INDEX"
        )
    )
    grouped: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[bool, list[str]]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        key = (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.INDEX_NAME))
        unique, columns = grouped.setdefault(key, (not bool(row.NON_UNIQUE), []))
        columns.append(str(row.COLUMN_NAME))
        grouped[key] = (unique, columns)
    return {
        key: (unique, tuple(columns))
        for key, (unique, columns) in grouped.items()
    }


def _normalized_generation_expression(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.lower().replace("_utf8mb4", "")
    return re.sub(r"[\\`()\s]", "", normalized)


def _structure_inventory(connection: Connection) -> dict[str, Any]:
    generated_rows = connection.execute(
        text(
            "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME, GENERATION_EXPRESSION "
            "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND GENERATION_EXPRESSION <> ''"
        )
    )
    generated = {
        (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME)): str(
            row.GENERATION_EXPRESSION
        )
        for row in generated_rows
    }
    indexes = _index_inventory(connection)
    installed_guards = tuple(
        sorted(
            f"{spec.table_name}.{spec.guard_column}"
            for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
            if _normalized_generation_expression(
                generated.get((spec.table_name, spec.guard_column), "")
            )
            == _normalized_generation_expression(
                f"CASE WHEN {spec.predicate} THEN 1 ELSE NULL END"
            )
        )
    )
    installed_guard_indexes = tuple(
        sorted(
            spec.index_name
            for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
            if indexes.get((spec.table_name, spec.index_name))
            == (True, (*spec.key_columns, spec.guard_column))
        )
    )
    source_hash_generated = _normalized_generation_expression(
        generated.get(("assets", "source_uri_sha256"), "")
    ) == _normalized_generation_expression("UNHEX(SHA2(source_uri, 256))")
    source_hash_index = indexes.get(
        ("assets", "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category")
    )
    source_uri_hash_contract = source_hash_generated and source_hash_index == (
        True,
        ("source_uri_sha256", "category_key"),
    )
    trigger_inventory = tuple(
        str(row.TRIGGER_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TRIGGER_NAME FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
                "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA=DATABASE() ORDER BY TRIGGER_NAME"
            )
        )
    )
    check_constraints = tuple(
        str(row.CONSTRAINT_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME FROM information_schema.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS "
                "WHERE CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA=DATABASE() ORDER BY CONSTRAINT_NAME"
            )
        )
    )
    return {
        "generated_unique_guards": installed_guards,
        "generated_guard_indexes": installed_guard_indexes,
        "source_uri_hash_contract": source_uri_hash_contract,
        "installed_invariant_triggers": tuple(
            sorted(set(trigger_inventory) & _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS)
        ),
        "installed_baseline_triggers": tuple(
            sorted(set(trigger_inventory) & _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES)
        ),
        "installed_triggers": trigger_inventory,
        "installed_check_constraints": check_constraints,
    }


def reconcile_mysql_databases(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
) -> MySQLReconciliationReport:
    """Stream and compare every mapped row without mutating either database."""

    from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401

    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise ReconciliationError("对账目标必须是 MySQL")
    try:
        resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(
            source
        )
        source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    except SnapshotImportError as exc:
        raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None
    try:
        quick_check_rows = source_connection.execute(
            "PRAGMA quick_check"
        ).fetchall()
        source_quick_check = ",".join(str(row[0]) for row in quick_check_rows)
        source_fk_violations = sum(
            1 for _row in source_connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check")
        )
        source_version = _source_schema_version(source_connection)
        source_compatibility_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        try:
            source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
                source_connection,
                Base.metadata,
            )
        except SnapshotImportError as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None
        reconciled_tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation] = {}
        with target.connect() as target_connection:
            health = assert_mysql_runtime_contract(target_connection)
            target_schema_value = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT DATABASE()")
            )
            target_schema = str(target_schema_value or "").strip()
            if not target_schema:
                raise ReconciliationError("MySQL 对账目标未选择 schema")
            schema_facts = _schema_facts(
                source_connection,
                target_connection,
                source_schema_version=source_compatibility_version,
                source_columns_by_table=source_columns_by_table,
            )
            for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
                columns = tuple(table.columns)
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                source_columns = source_columns_by_table.get(table.name)
                synthetic_defaults = {
                    name: value
                    for name, value in forward_compatible_column_defaults(
                        source_compatibility_version,
                        table.name,
                    ).items()
                    if source_columns is not None and name not in source_columns
                }
                source_rows = (
                    ()
                    if source_columns is None
                    else _source_rows(
                        source_connection,
                        table.name,
                        tuple(column.name for column in columns),
                        primary_key.name,
                        source_columns=source_columns,
                        synthetic_defaults=synthetic_defaults,
                    )
                )
                source_values = table_digest(
                    source_rows,
                    table_name=table.name,
                    columns=columns,
                    primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                )
                target_result = target_connection.execution_options(
                    stream_results=True,
                    yield_per=500,
                ).execute(select(table).order_by(primary_key))
                try:
                    target_values = table_digest(
                        target_result.mappings(),
                        table_name=table.name,
                        columns=columns,
                        primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                    )
                finally:
                    target_result.close()
                reconciled_tables[table.name] = TableReconciliation(
                    table_name=table.name,
                    source_row_count=source_values[0],
                    target_row_count=target_values[0],
                    source_primary_key_min=source_values[1],
                    source_primary_key_max=source_values[2],
                    target_primary_key_min=target_values[1],
                    target_primary_key_max=target_values[2],
                    source_null_counts=source_values[3],
                    target_null_counts=target_values[3],
                    source_hash=source_values[4],
                    target_hash=target_values[4],
                )
            target_fk_violations = _target_foreign_key_violations(
                target_connection
            )
            auto_increments = _auto_increment_states(
                target_connection,
                reconciled_tables,
            )
            target_schema_version = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations")
            )
            mysql_version = health.server_version
            structure = _structure_inventory(target_connection)
        return MySQLReconciliationReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            source_quick_check=source_quick_check,
            source_schema_version=source_version,
            target_schema=target_schema,
            target_schema_version=(
                None
                if target_schema_version is None
                else int(target_schema_version)
            ),
            target_mysql_version=mysql_version,
            source_foreign_key_violations=source_fk_violations,
            target_foreign_key_violations=target_fk_violations,
            schema_facts=schema_facts,
            tables=reconciled_tables,
            auto_increments=auto_increments,
            forward_compatible_source=(
                source_version is not None
                and target_schema_version is not None
                and _MIN_FORWARD_COMPATIBLE_SOURCE_VERSION
                <= source_version
                < int(target_schema_version)
                and int(target_schema_version)
                == max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
                and all(
                    fact["compatible"] for fact in schema_facts.values()
                )
            ),
            **structure,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        try:
            verify_source_unchanged(
                resolved_source,
                initial_stat=source_stat,
                initial_sha256=source_sha256,
            )
        except SnapshotImportError as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None


def assert_mysql_critical_invariants(
    report: MySQLReconciliationReport,
) -> None:
    failures: list[str] = []
    if report.source_quick_check != "ok":
        failures.append(
            f"SQLite quick_check={report.source_quick_check or 'empty'}"
        )
    if report.source_foreign_key_violations:
        failures.append(
            "SQLite foreign_key_check="
            f"{report.source_foreign_key_violations}"
        )
    target_fk_total = sum(report.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
    if target_fk_total:
        failures.append(f"MySQL foreign_key_violations={target_fk_total}")
    expected_tables = set(Base.metadata.tables)
    actual_tables = set(report.tables)
    if actual_tables != expected_tables:
        failures.append(
            "mapped_tables="
            f"{len(actual_tables)}/{len(expected_tables)}"
        )
    incompatible_schema = [
        name
        for name, fact in report.schema_facts.items()
        if not fact["compatible"]
    ]
    if incompatible_schema:
        failures.append(
            "schema=" + ",".join(sorted(incompatible_schema)[:10])
        )
    mismatched_tables = [
        name for name, table in report.tables.items() if not table.matches
    ]
    if mismatched_tables:
        failures.append(
            "tables=" + ",".join(sorted(mismatched_tables)[:10])
        )
    missing_critical = CRITICAL_TABLES - actual_tables
    if missing_critical:
        failures.append(
            "critical_missing=" + ",".join(sorted(missing_critical))
        )
    critical_mismatches = [
        name
        for name in CRITICAL_TABLES & actual_tables
        if not report.tables[name].matches
    ]
    if critical_mismatches:
        failures.append(
            "critical=" + ",".join(sorted(critical_mismatches))
        )
    expected_schema_version = max(
        migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS
    )
    if report.target_schema_version != expected_schema_version:
        failures.append(
            "schema_version="
            f"{report.target_schema_version}/{expected_schema_version}"
        )
    if (
        report.source_schema_version is not None
        and report.source_schema_version != report.target_schema_version
        and not report.forward_compatible_source
    ):
        failures.append(
            "source_schema_version="
            f"{report.source_schema_version}->{report.target_schema_version}"
        )
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(report.target_mysql_version)
    except ValueError:
        failures.append(f"mysql_version={report.target_mysql_version}")
    expected_guards = {
        f"{spec.table_name}.{spec.guard_column}"
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    if set(report.generated_unique_guards) != expected_guards:
        failures.append(
            f"generated_guards={len(report.generated_unique_guards)}/"
            f"{len(expected_guards)}"
        )
    expected_guard_indexes = {
        spec.index_name for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    if set(report.generated_guard_indexes) != expected_guard_indexes:
        failures.append(
            f"guard_indexes={len(report.generated_guard_indexes)}/"
            f"{len(expected_guard_indexes)}"
        )
    if not report.source_uri_hash_contract:
        failures.append("source_uri_hash_contract=missing")
    if set(report.installed_invariant_triggers) != _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS:
        failures.append(
            f"invariant_triggers={len(report.installed_invariant_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(_EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS)}"
        )
    if set(report.installed_baseline_triggers) != _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES:
        failures.append(
            f"baseline_triggers={len(report.installed_baseline_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(_BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES)}"
        )
    expected_all_triggers = _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS | _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES
    if set(report.installed_triggers) != expected_all_triggers:
        failures.append(
            f"all_triggers={len(report.installed_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(expected_all_triggers)}"
        )
    if set(report.installed_check_constraints) != _EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS:
        failures.append(
            f"check_constraints={len(report.installed_check_constraints)}/"
            f"{len(_EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS)}"
        )
    stale_auto_increments = [
        name for name, state in report.auto_increments.items() if not state.safe
    ]
    if stale_auto_increments:
        failures.append(
            "auto_increment="
            + ",".join(sorted(stale_auto_increments)[:10])
        )
    if failures:
        raise ReconciliationError("数据库对账未通过：" + "；".join(failures))

===== FILE backend/app/mysql_schema_clone.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import BinaryIO
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import URL
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection


class MySQLSchemaCloneError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when an isolated MySQL schema clone cannot be proven safe."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CandidateIdentityNames:
    app_username: str
    migration_username: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, repr=False)
class CandidateDatabaseAccounts:
    app_username: str
    app_password: str
    migration_username: str
    migration_password: str

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            "CandidateDatabaseAccounts("
            f"app_username={self.app_username!r}, "
            f"migration_username={self.migration_username!r}, "
            "passwords=<hidden>)"
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles:
    app: Path
    migration: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLSchemaCloneReport:
    source_schema: str
    target_schema: str
    stripped_definers: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLTableEvidence:
    row_count: int
    checksum: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLSchemaEvidence:
    schema: str
    migration: int
    tables: dict[str, MySQLTableEvidence]
    views: tuple[str, ...]
    trigger_count: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLCloneVerificationReport:
    table_count: int
    row_count: int
    evidence_hash: str


def _validated_identifier(value: str, *, description: str) -> str:
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", value) is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(f"{description}不是安全的 MySQL 标识符")
    return value


def _evidence_payload(evidence: MySQLSchemaEvidence) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "migration": evidence.migration,
        "tables": {
            name: {
                "row_count": fact.row_count,
                "checksum": fact.checksum,
            }
            for name, fact in sorted(evidence.tables.items())
        },
        "views": list(evidence.views),
        "trigger_count": evidence.trigger_count,
    }


def assert_exact_schema_clone(
    source: MySQLSchemaEvidence,
    target: MySQLSchemaEvidence,
) -> MySQLCloneVerificationReport:
    if source.migration != target.migration:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后 migration 不一致")
    if set(source.tables) != set(target.tables):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后表集合不一致")
    for table_name in sorted(source.tables):
        if source.tables[table_name] != target.tables[table_name]:
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                f"克隆表 {table_name} 的行数或 checksum 不一致"
            )
    if source.views != target.views:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后视图集合不一致")
    if source.trigger_count != target.trigger_count:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后触发器数量不一致")
    payload = _evidence_payload(source)
    evidence_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            sort_keys=True,
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return MySQLCloneVerificationReport(
        table_count=len(source.tables),
        row_count=sum(item.row_count for item in source.tables.values()),
        evidence_hash=evidence_hash,
    )


def mysql_schema_evidence(
    connection: Connection,
    schema: str,
) -> MySQLSchemaEvidence:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("schema 证据只能从 MySQL 读取")
    safe_schema = _validated_identifier(schema, description="schema")
    migration = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"SELECT MAX(version) FROM `{safe_schema}`.`schema_migrations`"
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    table_rows = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT TABLE_NAME FROM information_schema.TABLES "
        "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=%s AND TABLE_TYPE='BASE TABLE' ORDER BY TABLE_NAME",
        (safe_schema,),
    ).all()
    view_rows = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT TABLE_NAME FROM information_schema.TABLES "
        "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=%s AND TABLE_TYPE='VIEW' ORDER BY TABLE_NAME",
        (safe_schema,),
    ).all()
    trigger_count = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
            "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA=%s",
            (safe_schema,),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    tables: dict[str, MySQLTableEvidence] = {}
    for row in table_rows:
        table_name = _validated_identifier(str(row[0]), description="表名")
        row_count = int(
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{safe_schema}`.`{table_name}`"
            ).scalar_one()
        )
        checksum_row = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CHECKSUM TABLE `{safe_schema}`.`{table_name}`"
        ).one()
        if checksum_row[1] is None:
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                f"表 {table_name} 无法生成 MySQL checksum"
            )
        tables[table_name] = MySQLTableEvidence(
            row_count=row_count,
            checksum=int(checksum_row[1]),
        )
    views = tuple(
        _validated_identifier(str(row[0]), description="视图名")
        for row in view_rows
    )
    return MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema=safe_schema,
        migration=migration,
        tables=tables,
        views=views,
        trigger_count=trigger_count,
    )


def candidate_identity_names(target_schema: str) -> CandidateIdentityNames:
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    suffix = hashlib.sha256(target.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
    return CandidateIdentityNames(
        app_username=f"ls_app_{suffix}",
        migration_username=f"ls_mig_{suffix}",
    )


def prepare_candidate_database(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    source_schema: str,
    target_schema: str,
    accounts: CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
) -> None:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("候选数据库只能在 MySQL 上创建")
    source = _validated_identifier(source_schema, description="源 schema")
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    if source == target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 与目标 schema 不得相同")
    if not target.startswith("labelsystem_docker_candidate_"):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 不属于隔离 Docker 候选范围")
    expected_names = candidate_identity_names(target)
    if (
        accounts.app_username != expected_names.app_username
        or accounts.migration_username != expected_names.migration_username
    ):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("候选数据库身份未绑定到目标 schema")

    source_defaults = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT DEFAULT_CHARACTER_SET_NAME, DEFAULT_COLLATION_NAME "
        "FROM information_schema.SCHEMATA WHERE SCHEMA_NAME=%s",
        (source,),
    ).one_or_none()
    if source_defaults is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 不存在")
    charset, collation = (str(value) for value in source_defaults)
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]+", charset) is None or re.fullmatch(
        r"[A-Za-z0-9_]+", collation
    ) is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 字符集合同不安全")
    target_exists = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.SCHEMATA "
            "WHERE SCHEMA_NAME=%s",
            (target,),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    if target_exists:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 已存在，拒绝覆盖")
    identity_count = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM mysql.user WHERE User IN (%s, %s)",
            (accounts.app_username, accounts.migration_username),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    if identity_count:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 的隔离身份已存在，拒绝复用")

    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE DATABASE `{target}` CHARACTER SET {charset} COLLATE {collation}"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE USER '{accounts.app_username}'@'%%' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
        (accounts.app_password,),
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE USER '{accounts.migration_username}'@'%%' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
        (accounts.migration_password,),
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE "
        f"ON `{target}`.* TO '{accounts.app_username}'@'%%'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `{target}`.* "
        f"TO '{accounts.migration_username}'@'%%'"
    )


def _atomic_private_write(path: Path, content: str) -> Path:
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary_name, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
        os.chmod(path, 0o600)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary_name):
            os.unlink(temporary_name)
    return path.resolve(strict=True)


def validate_candidate_private_root(private_root: Path) -> Path:
    root = private_root.expanduser().absolute()
    if root == Path("/Volumes") or root.is_relative_to(Path("/Volumes")):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密不得写入外置卷")
    if root.is_symlink():
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密目录不得为符号链接")
    root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    resolved = root.resolve(strict=True)
    if resolved == Path("/Volumes") or resolved.is_relative_to(Path("/Volumes")):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密不得经符号链接写入外置卷")
    os.chmod(resolved, 0o700)
    return resolved


def write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
    private_root: Path,
    *,
    schema: str,
    host: str,
    port: int,
    accounts: CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
) -> CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles:
    target_schema = _validated_identifier(schema, description="目标 schema")
    if not host.strip() or any(character in host for character in "\r\n"):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("运行时数据库主机不合法")
    if port < 1 or port > 65535:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("运行时数据库端口不合法")
    root = validate_candidate_private_root(private_root)

    def rendered_url(username: str, password: str) -> str:
        return URL.create(
            "mysql+pymysql",
            username=username,
            password=password,
            host=host,
            port=port,
            database=target_schema,
            query={"charset": "utf8mb4"},
        ).render_as_string(hide_password=False)

    return CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles(
        app=_atomic_private_write(
            root / "app-database-url",
            rendered_url(accounts.app_username, accounts.app_password) + "\n",
        ),
        migration=_atomic_private_write(
            root / "migration-database-url",
            rendered_url(
                accounts.migration_username,
                accounts.migration_password,
            )
            + "\n",
        ),
    )


def _mysql_option_file(path: Path, database_url: URL) -> None:
    username = database_url.username
    password = database_url.password
    if not username or password is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端身份不完整")
    values = (username, str(password), database_url.host or "localhost")
    if any(any(character in value for character in "\r\n") for value in values):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端身份包含非法换行")
    lines = [
        "[client]",
        f"user={username}",
        f"password={password}",
        f"host={database_url.host or 'localhost'}",
    ]
    if database_url.port is not None:
        lines.append(f"port={database_url.port}")
    socket = database_url.query.get("unix_socket")
    if socket:
        if any(character in socket for character in "\r\n"):
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL socket 路径不合法")
        lines.extend(("protocol=socket", f"socket={socket}"))
    lines.append("default-character-set=utf8mb4")
    _atomic_private_write(path, "\n".join(lines) + "\n")


def _safe_client_error(error_file: object, *secrets_to_hide: str) -> str:
    error_file.seek(0)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    rendered = str(error_file.read())  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    for secret in secrets_to_hide:
        if secret:
            rendered = rendered.replace(secret, "<redacted>")
    return " ".join(rendered.split())[:500]


_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX = b"/*!50017 DEFINER="
_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PATTERN = re.compile(
    rb"/\*!50017 DEFINER=`[^`\r\n]{1,128}`@`[^`\r\n]{1,255}`\*/"
)


def _stream_without_mysql_definers(
    source: BinaryIO,
    target: BinaryIO,
) -> int:
    buffer = b""
    stripped = 0
    while True:
        chunk = source.read(1024 * 1024)
        reached_eof = not chunk
        buffer += chunk
        while buffer:
            start = buffer.find(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX)
            if start < 0:
                if reached_eof:
                    target.write(buffer)
                    buffer = b""
                else:
                    safe_length = max(
                        0,
                        len(buffer) - len(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX) + 1,
                    )
                    if safe_length:
                        target.write(buffer[:safe_length])
                        buffer = buffer[safe_length:]
                break
            if start:
                target.write(buffer[:start])
                buffer = buffer[start:]
            end = buffer.find(b"*/", len(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX))
            if end < 0:
                if reached_eof:
                    raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                        "mysqldump DEFINER 片段不完整，拒绝导入"
                    )
                if len(buffer) > 512:
                    raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                        "mysqldump DEFINER 片段超出安全长度"
                    )
                break
            token = buffer[: end + 2]
            if _MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PATTERN.fullmatch(token) is None:
                target.write(buffer[:1])
                buffer = buffer[1:]
                continue
            stripped += 1
            buffer = buffer[end + 2 :]
        if reached_eof:
            break
    return stripped


def clone_schema_with_clients(
    *,
    source_url: URL,
    target_url: URL,
    source_schema: str,
    target_schema: str,
    mysqldump: Path,
    mysql: Path,
) -> MySQLSchemaCloneReport:
    source = _validated_identifier(source_schema, description="源 schema")
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    if source == target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 与目标 schema 不得相同")
    if source_url.database != source or target_url.database != target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("客户端 URL 与 schema 合同不一致")
    for executable in (mysqldump, mysql):
        if not executable.is_file() or not os.access(executable, os.X_OK):
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端可执行文件不完整")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="labelsystem-mysql-clone-") as raw_dir:
        temporary_dir = Path(raw_dir)
        os.chmod(temporary_dir, 0o700)
        source_options = temporary_dir / "source-client.cnf"
        target_options = temporary_dir / "target-client.cnf"
        _mysql_option_file(source_options, source_url)
        _mysql_option_file(target_options, target_url)
        dump_command = (
            str(mysqldump),
            f"--defaults-extra-file={source_options}",
            "--single-transaction",
            "--quick",
            "--skip-lock-tables",
            "--routines",
            "--triggers",
            "--events",
            "--hex-blob",
            "--no-tablespaces",
            "--set-gtid-purged=OFF",
            "--default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            "--skip-comments",
            source,
        )
        restore_command = (
            str(mysql),
            f"--defaults-extra-file={target_options}",
            "--default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            target,
        )
        with (
            tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode="w+", encoding="utf-8") as dump_error,
            tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode="w+", encoding="utf-8") as restore_error,
        ):
            dump_process = subprocess.Popen(
                dump_command,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=dump_error,
            )
            if dump_process.stdout is None:
                dump_process.kill()
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 逻辑导出管道无法创建")
            restore_process = subprocess.Popen(
                restore_command,
                stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=restore_error,
            )
            if restore_process.stdin is None:
                dump_process.kill()
                restore_process.kill()
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 逻辑导入管道无法创建")
            restore_pipe_broken = False
            try:
                stripped_definers = _stream_without_mysql_definers(
                    dump_process.stdout,
                    restore_process.stdin,
                )
            except BrokenPipeError:
                restore_pipe_broken = True
                stripped_definers = 0
            finally:
                dump_process.stdout.close()
                try:
                    restore_process.stdin.close()
                except BrokenPipeError:
                    restore_pipe_broken = True
            if restore_pipe_broken and dump_process.poll() is None:
                dump_process.kill()
            restore_return_code = restore_process.wait()
            dump_return_code = dump_process.wait()
            if restore_return_code != 0:
                detail = _safe_client_error(
                    restore_error,
                    str(source_url.password or ""),
                    str(target_url.password or ""),
                )
                suffix = f"：{detail}" if detail else "；细节已隐藏"
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                    f"MySQL 目标 schema 导入失败{suffix}"
                )
            if dump_return_code != 0:
                detail = _safe_client_error(
                    dump_error,
                    str(source_url.password or ""),
                    str(target_url.password or ""),
                )
                suffix = f"：{detail}" if detail else "；细节已隐藏"
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                    f"MySQL 源 schema 逻辑导出失败{suffix}"
                )

    return MySQLSchemaCloneReport(
        source_schema=source,
        target_schema=target,
        stripped_definers=stripped_definers,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/nas_history_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import unicodedata
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .field_demand_contracts import record_asset_version
from .models import Asset, AssetVersion
from .nas_storage import normalize_nas_uri, nas_relative_path, resolve_nas_uri


class NasHistoryMigrationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkMatch:
    asset_id: int
    original_name: str
    stored_name: str
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    source_uri: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkIssue:
    asset_id: int
    stored_name: str
    reason: str
    detail: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkPlan:
    generated_at: str
    scan_roots: tuple[str, ...]
    matches: tuple[RelinkMatch, ...]
    issues: tuple[RelinkIssue, ...]
    summary: dict[str, int]
    plan_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkVerification:
    plan_hash: str
    ok: bool
    cleanup_stored_names: tuple[str, ...]
    issues: tuple[RelinkIssue, ...]


def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _safe_local_path(upload_dir: Path, stored_name: str) -> Path:
    root = upload_dir.resolve(strict=True)
    candidate = upload_dir / stored_name
    if candidate.is_symlink():
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("本地原图不能是符号链接")
    resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=False)
    try:
        resolved.relative_to(root)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("本地原图路径越过存储根目录") from exc
    return resolved


def _plan_hash_payload(
    *,
    generated_at: str,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    matches: Sequence[RelinkMatch],
    issues: Sequence[RelinkIssue],
    summary: dict[str, int],
) -> str:
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "nas-history-relink-plan-v1",
        "generated_at": generated_at,
        "scan_roots": list(scan_roots),
        "matches": [asdict(item) for item in matches],
        "issues": [asdict(item) for item in issues],
        "summary": summary,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _normalized_name(value: str) -> str:
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", value).replace("\\", "/").strip("/")


def _choose_candidate(original_name: str, candidates: Sequence[str]) -> str | None:
    if len(candidates) == 1:
        return candidates[0]
    original = _normalized_name(original_name)
    suffix_matches = [
        uri
        for uri in candidates
        if _normalized_name(str(nas_relative_path(uri))) == original
        or _normalized_name(str(nas_relative_path(uri))).endswith("/" + original)
    ]
    if len(suffix_matches) == 1:
        return suffix_matches[0]
    basename = Path(original).name
    basename_matches = [
        uri for uri in candidates if Path(str(nas_relative_path(uri))).name == basename
    ]
    return basename_matches[0] if len(basename_matches) == 1 else None


def _scan_nas_hashes(
    *,
    nas_root: Path,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    expected_by_size: dict[int, set[str]],
) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    hashes: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    visited: set[Path] = set()
    for raw_root in scan_roots:
        root_uri = normalize_nas_uri(f"nas://maps/{raw_root.strip('/')}")
        root_path = resolve_nas_uri(root_uri, nas_root)
        if not root_path.is_dir():
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"NAS 扫描目录不存在：{root_uri}")
        for current, directories, files in os.walk(root_path, followlinks=False):
            current_path = Path(current)
            directories[:] = [
                name for name in directories if not (current_path / name).is_symlink()
            ]
            for name in files:
                path = current_path / name
                if path.is_symlink() or path in visited:
                    continue
                visited.add(path)
                size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
                expected_hashes = expected_by_size.get(size_bytes)
                if not expected_hashes:
                    continue
                digest = _sha256_file(path)
                if digest not in expected_hashes:
                    continue
                relative = path.relative_to(nas_root).as_posix()
                hashes[digest].append(normalize_nas_uri(f"nas://maps/{relative}"))
    return hashes


def build_relink_plan(
    db: Session,
    *,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    generated_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> RelinkPlan:
    if not scan_roots:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("至少需要一个 NAS 扫描目录")
    assets = db.scalars(
        select(Asset)
        .where(func.coalesce(Asset.storage_backend, "local") == "local")
        .order_by(Asset.id)
    ).all()
    issues: list[RelinkIssue] = []
    eligible: list[Asset] = []
    expected_by_size: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for asset in assets:
        try:
            local_path = _safe_local_path(upload_dir, asset.stored_name)
        except NasHistoryMigrationError as exc:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "local_path_invalid", str(exc)))
            continue
