if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .workflow_result_finalizer import NativeEvaluationFact


class NativeBaselineRegressionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _json_object(value: str | None, *, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容损坏") from exc
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(code, "冻结 JSON 内容必须是对象")
    return parsed


def _expected_result(item: BaselineSetItem | BaselineRegressionItem) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if item.expected_result_json:
        expected = _json_object(
            item.expected_result_json,
            code="baseline_truth_invalid",
        )
        if expected:
            return expected
    if item.expected_level is not None:
        return {"level": item.expected_level}
    raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
        "baseline_truth_missing",
        "存量回归条目缺少等级或结构化真值",
        status_code=422,
    )


def _workflow_fixture(item: BaselineSetItem) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    snapshot = _json_object(
        item.asset_snapshot_json,
        code="baseline_asset_snapshot_invalid",
    )
    fixture = snapshot.get("workflow_fixture")
    if fixture is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(fixture, dict):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_prompt_fixture_invalid",
            "冻结提示词夹具必须是对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    prompt_outputs = fixture.get("prompt_outputs")
    if (
        fixture.get("schema_version") != "native-prompt-fixtures-v1"
        or not isinstance(prompt_outputs, dict)
        or not prompt_outputs
        or any(
            not isinstance(asset_key, str)
            or not asset_key.strip()
            or not isinstance(output, dict)
            for asset_key, output in prompt_outputs.items()
        )
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_prompt_fixture_invalid",
            "冻结提示词夹具版本或节点输出格式不正确",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return json.loads(canonical_json(fixture))


def _production_input_manifest(
    frozen_item: BaselineSetItem,
    truth: EffectiveBaselineTruth,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the exact frozen input shared by paid planning and execution."""

    input_manifest: dict[str, Any] = {
        "content_key": f"asset:{frozen_item.asset_id}",
        "asset_id": frozen_item.asset_id,
        "asset_sha256": frozen_item.asset.sha256,
        "baseline_set_item_id": frozen_item.id,
        "expected_result": truth.expected_result,
        "truth_source": truth.source,
        "truth_revision": truth.revision,
        "truth_revision_id": truth.revision_id,
        "truth_hash": truth.canonical_hash,
    }
    conflicting = sorted(set(truth.workflow_input) & set(input_manifest))
    if conflicting:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_workflow_input_conflict",
            "冻结工作流输入覆盖了系统字段：" + ",".join(conflicting),
            status_code=422,
        )
    input_manifest.update(truth.workflow_input)
    workflow_fixture = _workflow_fixture(frozen_item)
    if workflow_fixture is not None:
        input_manifest["workflow_fixture"] = workflow_fixture
    return input_manifest


def _run_fingerprint(
    category_key: str,
    items: Sequence[tuple[BaselineSetItem, EffectiveBaselineTruth]],
) -> str:
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "native-baseline-truth-v1",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "items": [
            {
                "baseline_set_item_id": item.id,
                "asset_id": item.asset_id,
                "asset_sha256": item.asset.sha256,
                "truth_contract_key": truth.truth_contract_key,
                "expected_result": truth.expected_result,
                "workflow_input": truth.workflow_input,
                "truth_revision": truth.revision,
                "truth_hash": truth.canonical_hash,
            }
            for item, truth in sorted(items, key=lambda row: row[0].id)
        ],
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _existing_idempotent_run(
    db: Session,
    baseline_set: BaselineSet,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> BaselineRegressionRun | None:
    runs = db.scalars(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.baseline_set_id == baseline_set.id
        )
    ).all()
    for run in runs:
        try:
            snapshot = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        if isinstance(snapshot, dict) and snapshot.get("idempotency_key") == idempotency_key:
            return run
    return None


def _initial_metrics(
    items: Sequence[tuple[BaselineSetItem, EffectiveBaselineTruth]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return compute_truth_metrics(
        {
            "status": "queued",
            "expected_result": truth.expected_result,
            "predicted_result": None,
        }
        for _item, truth in items
    )


def _effective_truths(
    db: Session,
    items: Sequence[BaselineSetItem],
) -> list[tuple[BaselineSetItem, EffectiveBaselineTruth]]:
    try:
        return [
            (item, resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item))
            for item in items
        ]
    except BaselineTruthError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc


def prompt_nodes_from_snapshot(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]:
    """Return prompt nodes from a frozen workflow without assuming A/B stages."""

    manifest: Mapping[str, Any] = snapshot
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    if isinstance(workflow, Mapping):
        nested_manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
        if isinstance(nested_manifest, Mapping):
            manifest = nested_manifest
    else:
        nested_manifest = snapshot.get("manifest")
        if isinstance(nested_manifest, Mapping):
            manifest = nested_manifest
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return ()
    prompts: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
    identities: set[str] = set()
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
        if executable is None:
            continue
        identity = str(
            node.get("node_instance_id") or node.get("key") or ""
        ).strip()
        if not identity or identity in identities:
            raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
                "native_prompt_identity_invalid",
                "冻结工作流的提示词节点标识为空或重复",
                status_code=422,
            )
        identities.add(identity)
        prompts.append(executable)
    return tuple(prompts)


def prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    """Expose frozen prompt identities without assuming an A/B pair."""

    identities: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node in prompt_nodes_from_snapshot(snapshot):
        node_id = str(
            node.get("node_instance_id") or node.get("key") or ""
        ).strip()
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        raw_contract = config.get("output_contract")
        contract_snapshot = config.get("output_contract_snapshot")
        output_schema = node.get("output_schema")
        output_contract_hash: str | None = None
        if isinstance(raw_contract, Mapping):
            output_contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(raw_contract).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
        elif isinstance(contract_snapshot, Mapping) and isinstance(
            contract_snapshot.get("contract_hash"), str
        ):
            output_contract_hash = str(contract_snapshot["contract_hash"])
        elif isinstance(output_schema, Mapping):
            output_contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(output_schema).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
        vision_hash = config.get("vision_policy_hash")
        identities.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_id,
                "output_contract_hash": output_contract_hash,
                "vision_policy_hash": (
                    str(vision_hash) if isinstance(vision_hash, str) else None
                ),
            }
        )
    return tuple(identities)


def field_contract_identities_from_snapshot(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
    """List frozen correction/dashboard field identities in display order."""

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(snapshot)
    identities: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str, str | None]] = set()
    for node in contract.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        metadata = node.get("metadata")
        metadata = metadata if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else {}
        identity = metadata.get("field_identity")
        if not isinstance(identity, Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = identity.get("node_instance_id")
        contract_hash = identity.get("output_contract_hash")
        field_path = identity.get("field_path")
        item_key = identity.get("item_key")
        if not all(
            isinstance(value, str) and value
            for value in (node_id, contract_hash, field_path)
        ):
            continue
        normalized_item_key = str(item_key) if isinstance(item_key, str) else None
        key = (node_id, contract_hash, field_path, normalized_item_key)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        identities.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_id,
                "output_contract_hash": contract_hash,
                "field_path": field_path,
                **(
                    {"item_key": normalized_item_key}
                    if normalized_item_key is not None
                    else {}
                ),
                "contract_field_key": str(node.get("node_key") or ""),
                "label": str(node.get("label") or field_path),
                "type": str(node.get("type") or "object"),
                "compatibility_key": str(
                    node.get("compatibility_key") or ""
                ),
            }
        )
    return tuple(identities)


def _model_configs_for_manifest(
    db: Session, manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[int, Any]:
    """取出 manifest 里每个提示词节点冻结的模型配置（含计价与 token 上限）。"""
    source_ids: set[int] = set()
    for node in prompt_nodes_from_snapshot(manifest):
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        model = config.get("model_snapshot")
        model = model if isinstance(model, Mapping) else {}
        source_id = model.get("source_model_config_id")
        if isinstance(source_id, int) and not isinstance(source_id, bool):
            source_ids.add(source_id)
    model_configs = {
        source_id: row
        for source_id in source_ids
        if (row := db.get(ModelConfig, source_id)) is not None
    }
    unavailable_models = [
        row
        for row in model_configs.values()
        if not secret_reference_available(row.encrypted_api_key)
    ]
    if unavailable_models:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "model_secret_unavailable",
            "真实模型密钥未保存或当前运行环境无法读取，请先在模型管理中重新保存并测试连接",
            status_code=422,
        )
    # 事前拦住模型漂移：否则执行器会把每条素材都判 model_config_snapshot_drift，
    # 一次模型都不调却整轮失败（run178：400/400）。
    drift = model_snapshot_issue_summary(model_snapshot_drift_issues(db, manifest))
    if drift:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "model_config_snapshot_drift",
            f"所选评测机制版本冻结的模型已过期：{drift}。"
            "请改选冻结了当前模型的机制版本，或到工作流管理器重新生成版本后再发起",
            status_code=409,
        )
    # 知识图谱路线编码升级（海外 is_single 1/2）后，旧版本的分支条件会把整体送进单体提示词
    route_issues = knowledge_graph_route_issues(manifest)
    if route_issues:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "kg_route_codes_stale",
            "所选评测机制版本冻结的知识图谱路线编码已过期："
            + "；".join(route_issues[:2])
            + "。请改选新生成的机制版本（启动时已按当前编码自动生成）后再发起",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return model_configs


def _manifest_of(workflow_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    workflow = workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
    workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
    return manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}


def _inference_route(db, definition_id, mode, *, manifest=None, dispatches=True):
    """按本轮选择解析并冻结一条推理路由。

    ``dispatches`` 表示这一轮会真的向供应商发请求（真实模型评测）。只有这种 run 才
    执行严格准入：缺接入点、缺批量价目一律在建 run/预估阶段就拒绝。快速模拟一次调用
    都不发、一分钱不花，同样按开关冻结模式，但不因为没绑批量接入点而被挡住。

    运营点名 batch（含省略 mode 时的默认 batch）时不冻结在线兜底：本轮全程批量，不
    能在批量尝试用尽或 24 小时到期后被恢复路径换成在线。只有 ``auto``——也就是「随
    平台默认策略走」——才保留共享的兜底行为。
    """

    from .doubao_endpoints import (
        DoubaoEndpointError,
        assert_explicit_route_ready,
        select_inference_route,
    )
    try:
        route = select_inference_route(
            db,
            definition_id,
            mode=mode,
            offline=True,
            require_endpoint=dispatches,
            allow_online_fallback=mode == 'auto',
        )
        if dispatches and manifest is not None:
            assert_explicit_route_ready(db, manifest, route, inference_mode=mode)
        return route
    except DoubaoEndpointError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code) from exc


def _real_execution_budget(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
    item_count: int,
    input_manifests: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
    inference_route: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[RealExecutionBudget, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
    workflow = workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
    workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
    manifest = manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}
    model_configs = _model_configs_for_manifest(db, manifest)
    from .batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
    try:
        model_configs = pricing_models_for_route(model_configs, inference_route)
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code) from exc
    output_expectations = historical_output_expectations(db, manifest)
    try:
        return (
            real_execution_budget_for_selection(
                manifest,
                item_count,
                model_configs=model_configs,
                input_manifests=(
                    list(input_manifests)
                    if input_manifests is not None
                    else None
                ),
                output_expectations=output_expectations,
            ),
            real_execution_node_limits(
                manifest,
                model_configs=model_configs,
                input_manifests=(
                    list(input_manifests)
                    if input_manifests is not None
                    else None
                ),
                output_expectations=output_expectations,
            ),
        )
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc


def _pricing_preview_nodes(
    limits: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Expose frozen price evidence without returning arbitrary model state."""

    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node_instance_id, raw in sorted(limits.items()):
        pricing_snapshot = raw.get("pricing_snapshot")
        pricing_snapshot = (
            pricing_snapshot if isinstance(pricing_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        )
        raw_tiers = pricing_snapshot.get("tiers")
        pricing_tiers = [
            {
                "tier_key": tier.get("tier_key"),
                "min_input_tokens": tier.get("min_input_tokens"),
                "max_input_tokens": tier.get("max_input_tokens"),
                "input_micros_per_1k": tier.get("input_micros_per_1k"),
                "cached_input_micros_per_1k": tier.get(
                    "cached_input_micros_per_1k"
                ),
                "output_micros_per_1k": tier.get("output_micros_per_1k"),
            }
            for tier in raw_tiers
            if isinstance(tier, Mapping)
        ] if isinstance(raw_tiers, list) else []
        result.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
                "provider": raw.get("provider"),
                "model_id": raw.get("model_id"),
                "input_tokens_per_request": raw.get("max_input_tokens"),
                "output_tokens_per_request": raw.get("max_output_tokens"),
                "estimated_cost_micros_per_request": raw.get(
                    "estimated_cost_micros"
                ),
                "pricing_version": raw.get("pricing_version"),
                "pricing_tier_key": raw.get("pricing_tier_key"),
                "expected_cost_basis": "cold_cost_upper_bound",
                "expected_input_tokens_per_request": raw.get(
                    "expected_input_tokens_per_request"
                ),
                "expected_output_tokens_per_request": raw.get(
                    "expected_output_tokens_per_request"
                ),
                "expected_cost_micros_per_request": raw.get(
                    "expected_cost_micros_per_request"
                ),
                "expected_pricing_tier_key": raw.get(
                    "expected_pricing_tier_key"
                ),
                "expected_request_count": raw.get(
                    "expected_request_count"
                ),
                "expected_input_tokens": raw.get("expected_input_tokens"),
                "expected_output_tokens": raw.get("expected_output_tokens"),
                "expected_cost_micros": raw.get("expected_cost_micros"),
                "expected_tier_buckets": raw.get(
                    "expected_tier_buckets"
                ),
                "configured_max_attempts": raw.get(
                    "configured_max_attempts"
                ),
                "retry_reserve_request_count": raw.get(
                    "retry_reserve_request_count"
                ),
                "expected_output_basis": raw.get("expected_output_basis"),
                "pricing_tiers": pricing_tiers,
                "source_url": pricing_snapshot.get("source_url"),
                "effective_at": pricing_snapshot.get("effective_at"),
            }
        )
    return result


def _expected_output_source(
    limits: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> str:
    """汇总各节点「预计输出」的依据，供预览一句话说明。"""

    sources = set()
    for raw in limits.values():
        basis = raw.get("expected_output_basis")
        if isinstance(basis, Mapping):
            sources.add(str(basis.get("source") or "output_cap"))
    if not sources:
        return "output_cap"
    if sources == {"output_cap"}:
        return "output_cap"
    if "output_cap" in sources:
        return "mixed"
    return "history"


def cancel_native_baseline_runs(
    db: Session, *, baseline_run_ids: Sequence[int]
) -> int:
    """取消走新工作流路径的存量回归（这些条目没有 evaluation_job）。

    旧取消接口只处理 evaluation_jobs，而 native workflow 的回归条目 job_id 全为
    NULL——运营点「取消本轮」接口返回成功、实际影响 0 条，模型继续调、钱继续花
    （2026-08-28 run135 就是这样，最后只能手工改库止血）。

    顺序有讲究：先掐调度（worker 不再认领新步骤），再收未开始的步骤与运行，
    最后判条目。正在执行中的步骤留给它自然收尾，避免制造写到一半的僵尸状态。
    返回受影响的条目数。
    """
    run_ids = [int(run_id) for run_id in baseline_run_ids]
    if not run_ids:
        return 0
    production_ids = sorted(
        {
            int(production_id)
            for production_id in db.scalars(
                select(BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id).where(
                    BaselineRegressionItem.run_id.in_(run_ids),
                    BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id.is_not(None),
                )
            )
            if production_id is not None
        }
    )
    if production_ids:
        # 与模型调用预留保持同一锁顺序：run -> attempt -> invocation。
        # Worker 先预留时，取消会等它提交后把预留收口；取消先执行时，Worker
        # 会等到终态提交后因 model_run_terminal_state 被拒绝。
        db.scalars(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id.in_(production_ids))
            .order_by(ProductionRun.id)
            .with_for_update()
            .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
        ).all()
        locked_attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id.in_(production_ids))
            .order_by(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.id,
            )
            .with_for_update()
            .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
        ).all()
        attempt_ids = [attempt.id for attempt in locked_attempts]
        fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs(
            db,
            production_ids,
            error_message=(
                "运营取消运行，模型供应商执行结果无法确认"
            ),
        )
        # 1) 止血：让 worker 不再认领这些运行的任何排队步骤。
        db.execute(
            update(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id.in_(attempt_ids),
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status.in_(("queued", "leased")),
            )
            .values(status="canceled")
        )
        # 2) 未开始的步骤按跳过收束。
        db.execute(
            update(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id.in_(production_ids),
                ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(("pending", "retryable")),
            )
            .values(status="skipped")
        )
        # 执行中的步骤也必须收束成终态：它们的 dispatch 已被取消，worker 一旦
        # 换代（部署重建容器）就永远没人收尾，而认领逻辑把 leased/running 的
        # 步骤计入并发占用——run135 取消后残留的 8 个 running 僵尸恰好占满
        # global_limit=8，把下一轮 run136 整个饿死在队列里。
        db.execute(
            update(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id.in_(production_ids),
                ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(("leased", "running")),
            )
            .values(status="failed")
        )
        # 3) 运行本体标记取消。
        db.execute(
            update(ProductionRun)
            .where(
                ProductionRun.id.in_(production_ids),
                ProductionRun.status.in_(
                    ("planned", "queued", "running", "paused", "retryable")
                ),
            )
            .values(status="canceled")
        )
    # 4) 回归条目按「运营取消」判定——与旧路径取消同一口径，看板与纠偏都认它。
    affected = 0
    # 只认领没有 evaluation_job 的条目：老路径条目由 job 取消循环负责判定与
    # 记账，这里再动一遍会双重计数、也会绕过 job 侧的资产状态回写。
    items = db.scalars(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.run_id.in_(run_ids),
            BaselineRegressionItem.status.in_(("queued", "running")),
            BaselineRegressionItem.job_id.is_(None),
        )
    ).all()
    for item in items:
        item.status = "failed"
        item.error_message = "technical:operator_canceled"
        affected += 1
    # 5) run 汇总收到终态，看板立即停止轮询。
    if affected:
        for run in db.scalars(
            select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
                BaselineRegressionRun.id.in_(run_ids),
                BaselineRegressionRun.status == "running",
            )
        ).all():
            run.status = "failed"
            run.failed = run.total
            run.completed = run.total
    return affected


def _baseline_anchor_overlaps(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], selected: Sequence[BaselineSetItem]
) -> list[int]:
    """Record exact content/ID overlap without changing anchors or truth."""
    hashes = {item.asset.sha256 for item in selected}
    asset_ids = {item.asset_id for item in selected}
    overlaps: set[int] = set()
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        config = node.get("config", {})
        if node.get("kind") != "rule_module" or (
            config.get("asset_key") != "anchor_calibration"
            and config.get("module_type_key") != "anchor_calibration"
        ):
            continue
        component = config.get("component_snapshot", {})
        content = _json_object(
            component.get("content_json", "{}"),
            code="baseline_anchor_snapshot_invalid",
        )
        if content.get("enabled", True) is False:
            continue
        for anchor in content.get("anchors", []):
            if anchor.get("sha256") in hashes or anchor.get("asset_id") in asset_ids:
                overlaps.add(anchor["asset_id"])
    return sorted(overlaps)


def _assert_baseline_anchor_isolation(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any], selected: Sequence[BaselineSetItem]
) -> None:
    """Retained for explicitly independent holdout experiments."""
    overlaps = _baseline_anchor_overlaps(manifest, selected)
    if overlaps:
        names = "、".join(str(value) for value in overlaps[:10])
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_anchor_overlap",
            f"评分锚点与待评素材内容重合（共{len(overlaps)}张，锚点素材：{names}）。"
            "请新建机制并改用基准集之外的人工标注锚点，再启动回归；不要删除待评样本。",
            status_code=422,
        )


def _baseline_anchor_usage(manifest, selected, evaluation_purpose):
    if evaluation_purpose not in {'golden_calibration', 'independent_holdout'}:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError('baseline_evaluation_purpose_invalid',
            '评测用途必须是黄金集校准或独立留出验证', status_code=422)
    if evaluation_purpose == 'independent_holdout':
        _assert_baseline_anchor_isolation(manifest, selected)
    # User decision 2026-09-24: golden images may serve as calibration anchors.
    # Keep overlap visible in the immutable run, never advertise holdout proof.
    overlaps = _baseline_anchor_overlaps(manifest, selected)
    return dict(policy_version='golden-calibration-20260924',
        evaluation_purpose=evaluation_purpose, overlapping_anchor_asset_ids=overlaps,
        overlapping_anchor_count=len(overlaps),
        overlap_allowed=evaluation_purpose=='golden_calibration')


def preview_real_execution_budget(
    db: Session,
    *,
    baseline_set: Any,
    workflow_version: Any,
    item_count: int,
    inference_mode: str = "auto",
    evaluation_purpose: str = "golden_calibration",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按当前选择估算一轮真实评测的用量与费用，并给出服务端硬上限。

    与建 run 用的是同一套算法，但不拦截：超限时照样返回数字，让运营在点按钮
    之前就看清「要花多少钱」以及「卡在哪一项上限」。
    """
    try:
        mechanism = resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
            db,
            category_key=baseline_set.category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
            runtime_context={
                "input_manifest": {
                    "baseline_set_id": baseline_set.id,
                    "item_count": item_count,
                }
            },
        )
    except StockMechanismError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code
        ) from exc

    workflow = mechanism.snapshot.get("workflow")
    workflow = workflow if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest")
    manifest = manifest if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}
    selected = list(baseline_set.items)[:item_count]
    anchor_usage = _baseline_anchor_usage(manifest, selected, evaluation_purpose)
    route = _inference_route(
        db, workflow_version.workflow_definition_id, inference_mode, manifest=manifest
    )
    from .batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
    try:
        model_configs = pricing_models_for_route(_model_configs_for_manifest(db, manifest), route)
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code) from exc
    if len(selected) != item_count:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_items_count_mismatch",
            "存量回归预估的素材数量与冻结基准集不一致",
            status_code=409,
        )
    preview_inputs = [
        _production_input_manifest(item, truth)
        for item, truth in _effective_truths(db, selected)
    ]
    output_expectations = historical_output_expectations(db, manifest)
    try:
        budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            manifest,
            item_count,
            model_configs=model_configs,
            input_manifests=preview_inputs,
            enforce=False,
            output_expectations=output_expectations,
        )
        node_limits = real_execution_node_limits(
            manifest,
            model_configs=model_configs,
            input_manifests=preview_inputs,
            output_expectations=output_expectations,
        )
    except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code
        ) from exc

    caps = {
        "max_requests": MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
        "max_input_tokens": MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
        "max_output_tokens": MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
        "max_cost_micros": MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS,
    }
    exceeded = [
        name
        for name, expected, cap in (
            ("requests", budget.expected_requests, MAX_REAL_REQUESTS),
            (
                "input_tokens",
                budget.expected_input_tokens,
                MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
            ),
            (
                "output_tokens",
                budget.expected_output_tokens,
                MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
            ),
            ("cost", budget.expected_cost_micros, MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS),
        )
        if expected > cap
    ]
    return {
        "item_count": item_count,
        "anchor_usage": anchor_usage,
        "expected": {
            "requests": budget.expected_requests,
            "input_tokens": budget.expected_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": budget.expected_output_tokens,
            "cost_micros": budget.expected_cost_micros,
            "cost_basis": "cold_cost_upper_bound",
            "image_tokens": None,
            "image_tokens_included_in_input": True,
        },
        "retry_reserve": {
            "requests": max(0, budget.max_requests - budget.expected_requests),
            "input_tokens": max(
                0, budget.max_input_tokens - budget.expected_input_tokens
            ),
            "output_tokens": max(
                0, budget.max_output_tokens - budget.expected_output_tokens
            ),
            "cost_micros": max(
                0, budget.max_cost_micros - budget.expected_cost_micros
            ),
        },
        "node_pricing": _pricing_preview_nodes(node_limits),
        "expected_output_source": _expected_output_source(node_limits),
        "caps": caps,
        "exceeded": exceeded,
        "within_caps": not exceeded,
        "inference_route": route,
        "doubao_endpoint": _doubao_endpoint_preview(db, manifest, workflow_version, route),
    }


def _doubao_endpoint_preview(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    workflow_version: Any,
    route: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """本轮真实调用将走哪个豆包接入点；路由开关打开且缺失时明确阻塞。"""

    from .doubao_endpoints import (
        endpoint_binding_summary,
        endpoint_readiness_blocker,
        endpoint_routing_enabled,
        manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint,
    )

    definition_id = getattr(workflow_version, "workflow_definition_id", None)
    return {
        "routing_enabled": endpoint_routing_enabled(db),
        "required": manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, manifest),
        "binding": ({**(endpoint_binding_summary(db, definition_id) or {}), **route, "display_name": "批量推理" if route["mode"] == "batch" else "在线推理"} if route.get("endpoint_id") else None),
        "blocker": None if route.get("endpoint_id") else endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, definition_id, inference_mode=route["mode"]),
    }


def create_native_baseline_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    baseline_set: BaselineSet,
    workflow_version: WorkflowVersion,
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
    items: Sequence[BaselineSetItem] | None = None,
    model_execution_mode: str = "simulated",
    real_execution_confirmed: bool = False,
    inference_mode: str = "auto",
    cost_cap_micros: int | None = None,
    evaluation_purpose: str = "golden_calibration",
) -> BaselineRegressionRun:
    """Create one aggregate run and one native ProductionRun per frozen item."""

    selected = list(items if items is not None else baseline_set.items)
    if cost_cap_micros is not None and (
        isinstance(cost_cap_micros, bool)
        or not isinstance(cost_cap_micros, int)
        or not 0 < cost_cap_micros <= MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS
        or model_execution_mode != "real"
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "real_execution_cost_cap_invalid",
            "独立费用上限必须为真实评测的正整数微元，且不得超过平台硬限额",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if model_execution_mode not in {"simulated", "real"}:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "model_execution_mode_invalid",
            "模型执行方式只能是快速模拟或真实模型评测",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if model_execution_mode == "real" and not real_execution_confirmed:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "real_execution_confirmation_required",
            "真实模型评测需要明确确认费用和素材外发",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if not selected:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_items_empty",
            "空基准集不能创建存量回归",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if any(item.baseline_set_id != baseline_set.id for item in selected):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_items_invalid",
            "所选条目不属于当前冻结基准集",
            status_code=422,
        )
    try:
        mechanism = resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
            db,
            category_key=baseline_set.category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
            runtime_context={
                "input_manifest": {
                    "baseline_set_id": baseline_set.id,
                    "item_count": len(selected),
                }
            },
        )
    except StockMechanismError as exc:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    effective_truths = _effective_truths(db, selected)
    truth_fingerprint = _run_fingerprint(
        baseline_set.category_key,
        effective_truths,
    )
    existing = _existing_idempotent_run(db, baseline_set, idempotency_key)
    if existing is not None:
        existing_snapshot = _json_object(
            existing.execution_snapshot_json,
            code="native_baseline_snapshot_invalid",
        )
        if (
            existing.workflow_version_id != workflow_version.id
            or existing.baseline_set_fingerprint != truth_fingerprint
            or existing_snapshot.get("workflow_snapshot_hash")
            != mechanism.snapshot_hash
            or existing_snapshot.get("model_execution_mode", "simulated")
            != model_execution_mode
            or existing_snapshot.get("inference_mode", "auto") != inference_mode
            or existing_snapshot.get("requested_cost_cap_micros") != cost_cap_micros
            or existing_snapshot.get("evaluation_purpose", "golden_calibration") != evaluation_purpose
        ):
            raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
                "native_baseline_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同幂等键绑定的工作流或冻结真值不一致",
            )
        return existing
    anchor_usage = _baseline_anchor_usage(_manifest_of(mechanism.snapshot), selected, evaluation_purpose)
    running = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.baseline_set_id == baseline_set.id,
            BaselineRegressionRun.status == "running",
        )
    )
    if running is not None:
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "baseline_run_in_progress",
            "该基准集上一轮仍在运行",
        )
    previous = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun)
        .where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.baseline_set_id == baseline_set.id,
            BaselineRegressionRun.status.in_(
                {"completed", "partial_failed", "failed"}
            ),
        )
        .order_by(BaselineRegressionRun.sequence_no.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    sequence_no = (previous.sequence_no + 1) if previous is not None else 1
    truth_snapshot = [
        {
            "baseline_set_item_id": item.id,
            "asset_id": item.asset_id,
            "truth_contract_key": truth.truth_contract_key,
            "expected_level": truth.expected_level,
            "expected_result": truth.expected_result,
            "workflow_input": truth.workflow_input,
            "truth_source": truth.source,
            "truth_revision": truth.revision,
            "truth_revision_id": truth.revision_id,
            "truth_hash": truth.canonical_hash,
        }
        for item, truth in sorted(effective_truths, key=lambda row: row[0].id)
    ]
    production_inputs = [
        _production_input_manifest(item, truth)
        for item, truth in effective_truths
    ]
    route = _inference_route(
        db,
        workflow_version.workflow_definition_id,
        inference_mode,
        manifest=_manifest_of(mechanism.snapshot),
        dispatches=model_execution_mode == "real",
    )
    real_execution_plan = (
        _real_execution_budget(
            db,
            workflow_snapshot=mechanism.snapshot,
            item_count=len(selected),
            input_manifests=production_inputs,
            inference_route=route,
        )
        if model_execution_mode == "real"
        else None
    )
    real_budget = (
        real_execution_plan[0] if real_execution_plan is not None else None
    )
    if real_budget is not None and cost_cap_micros is not None:
        real_budget = replace(
            real_budget,
            max_cost_micros=min(real_budget.max_cost_micros, cost_cap_micros),
        )
    real_node_limits = (
        real_execution_plan[1] if real_execution_plan is not None else None
    )
    prompt_contract_identities = list(
        prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(mechanism.snapshot)
    )
    field_contract_identities = list(
        field_contract_identities_from_snapshot(mechanism.snapshot)
    )
    execution_snapshot = {
        "schema_version": "native-baseline-execution-v1",
        "evaluation_purpose": evaluation_purpose,
        "anchor_usage": anchor_usage,
        "inference_mode": inference_mode,
        "inference_route": route,
        "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
        "execution_engine": "native_workflow",
        "model_execution_mode": model_execution_mode,
        "requested_cost_cap_micros": cost_cap_micros,
        "real_execution_budget": (
            real_budget.as_dict() if real_budget is not None else None
        ),
        "real_execution_node_limits": real_node_limits,
        "category_key": baseline_set.category_key,
        "workflow_snapshot": mechanism.snapshot,
        "workflow_snapshot_hash": mechanism.snapshot_hash,
        "prompt_node_ids": [
            item["node_instance_id"] for item in prompt_contract_identities
        ],
        "prompt_contract_identities": prompt_contract_identities,
        "field_contract_identities": field_contract_identities,
        "truth_snapshot": truth_snapshot,
    }
    correction_contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        mechanism.snapshot
    )
    if model_execution_mode != "real":
        for contract_node in correction_contract.get("nodes", []):
            if not isinstance(contract_node, dict):
                continue
            metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
            if isinstance(metadata, dict):
                metadata.pop("paid_rerun_required", None)
        correction_contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(
            correction_contract
        )
    run = BaselineRegressionRun(
        baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
        sequence_no=sequence_no,
        previous_run_id=previous.id if previous is not None else None,
        strategy_bundle_id=None,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
        execution_engine="native_workflow",
        category_key=baseline_set.category_key,
        strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
        execution_snapshot_json=canonical_json(execution_snapshot),
        correction_contract_json=canonical_json(correction_contract),
        correction_contract_hash=correction_contract["contract_hash"],
        baseline_set_fingerprint=truth_fingerprint,
        status="running",
        total=len(selected),
        completed=0,
        valid_predictions=0,
        failed=0,
        metrics_json=canonical_json(_initial_metrics(effective_truths)),
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    run_items: list[BaselineRegressionItem] = []
    for frozen_item, truth in effective_truths:
        run_item = BaselineRegressionItem(
            run_id=run.id,
            baseline_set_item_id=frozen_item.id,
            asset_id=frozen_item.asset_id,
            expected_level=truth.expected_level,
            expected_result_json=canonical_json(truth.expected_result),
            truth_contract_key=truth.truth_contract_key,
            status="queued",
            result_snapshot_json="{}",
        )
        db.add(run_item)
        run_items.append(run_item)
    db.flush()
    for (frozen_item, _truth), run_item, input_manifest in zip(
        effective_truths,
        run_items,
        production_inputs,
        strict=True,
    ):
        production = create_production_run(
            db,
            ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
                idempotency_key=(
                    f"{idempotency_key}:item:{run_item.id}:"
                    f"{workflow_version.canonical_hash}"
                ),
                category_key=baseline_set.category_key,
                queue_class="validation",
                runtime_context={
                    "run_mode": "stock_regression",
                    "inference_route": route,
                    "baseline_run_id": run.id,
                    "baseline_item_id": run_item.id,
                    "truth_contract_key": run_item.truth_contract_key,
                    "real_execution_confirmed": (
                        model_execution_mode == "real"
                        and real_execution_confirmed
                    ),
                    "real_execution_budget": (
                        real_budget.as_dict()
                        if real_budget is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                    "real_execution_node_limits": real_node_limits,
                },
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
                owner="baseline-regression",
                reason="原生工作流存量回归",
                source_type="baseline_regression_item",
                source_id=run_item.id,
                environment="dry_run",
                model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode,
            ),
            actor=actor,
        )
        run_item.production_run_id = production.id
    db.flush()
    return run


def _flatten_fields(value: Mapping[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    flattened: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, child in value.items():
        path = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key)
        if isinstance(child, Mapping):
            flattened.update(_flatten_fields(child, path))
        else:
            flattened[path] = child
    return flattened


def truth_field_differences(
    expected: Mapping[str, Any],
    predicted: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[str]:
    """Return stable leaf paths whose frozen truth and native result differ."""

    expected_fields = _flatten_fields(expected)
    predicted_fields = _flatten_fields(predicted)
    missing = object()
    return [
        field
        for field in sorted(expected_fields)
        if _known_truth_value(expected_fields[field])
        if (
            predicted_fields.get(field, missing) is missing
            or canonical_json(expected_fields[field])
            != canonical_json(predicted_fields[field])
        )
    ]


def _tokens(value: Any) -> set[str]:
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return {canonical_json(item) for item in value}
    if value is None:
        return set()
    return {canonical_json(value)}


def _known_truth_value(value: Any) -> bool:
    return not (isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip())


def _ratio(numerator: int, denominator: int) -> float:
    return numerator / denominator if denominator else 0.0


def compute_truth_metrics(items: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows = [dict(item) for item in items]
    total = len(rows)
    # 每条的工作流运行状态（succeeded/failed/canceled，其余算 pending）在这里一次算好并随
    # metrics_json 持久化：/api/baseline-sets 概览每次为分类下所有 run 重算这组计数时，
    # 要流式 JSON_EXTRACT 每条 31 KB 的结果快照（09-30 实测每分钟 168 次 × 233 ms）。
    workflow_status_counts = {"succeeded": 0, "failed": 0, "canceled": 0, "pending": 0}
    for row in rows:
        state = row.get("workflow_status")
        workflow_status_counts[state if state in ("succeeded", "failed", "canceled") else "pending"] += 1
    terminal = sum(
        row.get("status") in {"completed", "failed", "manual_required"}
        for row in rows
    )
    failed = sum(row.get("status") == "failed" for row in rows)
    manual_required = sum(
        row.get("status") == "manual_required" for row in rows
    )
    valid_rows = [
        row
        for row in rows
        if row.get("status") == "completed"
        and isinstance(row.get("predicted_result"), Mapping)
    ]
    known_field_count = 0
    pending_truth_field_count = 0
    pending_truth_fields: set[str] = set()
    for row in rows:
        expected = row.get("expected_result")
        if not isinstance(expected, Mapping):
            continue
        expected_fields = _flatten_fields(
            {key: value for key, value in expected.items() if key != "level"}
        )
        for field, value in expected_fields.items():
            if _known_truth_value(value):
                known_field_count += 1
            else:
                pending_truth_field_count += 1
                pending_truth_fields.add(field)
    field_counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    aggregate = {
        "compared": 0,
        "correct": 0,
        "true_positive": 0,
        "false_positive": 0,
        "false_negative": 0,
    }
    level_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    image_grade_pairs: list[tuple[list[Any], list[Any]]] = []
    for row in valid_rows:
        expected = row.get("expected_result")
        predicted = row.get("predicted_result")
        if not isinstance(expected, Mapping) or not isinstance(predicted, Mapping):
            continue
        # 图片维度：一个基准项里有几十张图，字段级的集合比对只能告诉你「这一项
        # 有没有全对」。逐图档位单独汇总，运营要看的是每张图判对了没有，
        # 以及判错时是判高了还是判低了。
        if isinstance(expected.get("images"), list):
            image_grade_pairs.append(
                (expected["images"], predicted.get("images") or [])
            )
        expected_level = expected.get("level")
        if expected_level is not None:
            level_rows.append(
                {
                    "status": "completed",
                    "expected_level": expected_level,
                    "predicted_level": predicted.get("level"),
                }
            )
        expected_fields = _flatten_fields(
            {key: value for key, value in expected.items() if key != "level"}
        )
        expected_fields = {
            key: value
            for key, value in expected_fields.items()
            if _known_truth_value(value)
        }
        predicted_fields = _flatten_fields(
            {key: value for key, value in predicted.items() if key != "level"}
        )
        for field in sorted(expected_fields):
            counts = field_counts.setdefault(
                field,
                {
                    "compared": 0,
                    "correct": 0,
                    "true_positive": 0,
                    "false_positive": 0,
                    "false_negative": 0,
                },
            )
            expected_tokens = _tokens(expected_fields.get(field))
            predicted_tokens = _tokens(predicted_fields.get(field))
            correct = expected_tokens == predicted_tokens
            counts["compared"] += 1
            counts["correct"] += int(correct)
            counts["true_positive"] += len(expected_tokens & predicted_tokens)
            counts["false_positive"] += len(predicted_tokens - expected_tokens)
            counts["false_negative"] += len(expected_tokens - predicted_tokens)
            aggregate["compared"] += 1
            aggregate["correct"] += int(correct)
            aggregate["true_positive"] += len(
                expected_tokens & predicted_tokens
            )
            aggregate["false_positive"] += len(
                predicted_tokens - expected_tokens
            )
            aggregate["false_negative"] += len(
                expected_tokens - predicted_tokens
            )
    metrics_by_field = {
        field: {
            **counts,
            "accuracy": _ratio(counts["correct"], counts["compared"]),
            "precision": _ratio(
                counts["true_positive"],
                counts["true_positive"] + counts["false_positive"],
            ),
            "recall": _ratio(
                counts["true_positive"],
                counts["true_positive"] + counts["false_negative"],
            ),
        }
        for field, counts in sorted(field_counts.items())
    }
    field_aggregate = {
        "compared": aggregate["compared"],
        "correct": aggregate["correct"],
        "accuracy": _ratio(aggregate["correct"], aggregate["compared"]),
        "precision": _ratio(
            aggregate["true_positive"],
            aggregate["true_positive"] + aggregate["false_positive"],
        ),
        "recall": _ratio(
            aggregate["true_positive"],
            aggregate["true_positive"] + aggregate["false_negative"],
        ),
        "true_positive": aggregate["true_positive"],
        "false_positive": aggregate["false_positive"],
        "false_negative": aggregate["false_negative"],
    }
    # 真值口径由基准集本身决定，与本轮跑没跑出结果无关：全部失败时也必须如实
    # 告诉前端「这是等级回归」，否则界面会退回知识图谱的字段看板（2026-08-28
    # run133 全灭时，灵感图的回归页就显示成了「知识图谱按字段核对」）。
    structured_truth = any(
        isinstance(row.get("expected_result"), Mapping)
        and any(key != "level" for key in row["expected_result"])
        for row in rows
    )
    short_circuit_rows = [
        row for row in valid_rows
        if isinstance(row.get("predicted_result"), Mapping)
        and isinstance(row["predicted_result"].get("short_circuit_decision"), Mapping)
    ]
    saved_known = [
        row["predicted_result"]["short_circuit_decision"].get("estimated_saved_cost_micros")
        for row in short_circuit_rows
    ]
    saved_total = sum(int(v) for v in saved_known if isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool))
    from .truth_metric_groups import separate_truth_metric_groups
    return separate_truth_metric_groups({
        "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
        "workflow_status_counts": workflow_status_counts,
        "truth_kind": "structured_fields" if structured_truth else "level",
        "total": total,
        # 规则短路命中数与估算省下的模型调用费（同版本同节点历史均值估算，不是账单）
        "short_circuit_count": len(short_circuit_rows),
        "estimated_saved_cost_micros": saved_total,
        "completed": terminal,
        "pending": total - terminal,
        "valid_predictions": len(valid_rows),
        "failed": failed,
        "manual_required": manual_required,
        "coverage": _ratio(len(valid_rows), total),
        "known_field_count": known_field_count,
        "pending_truth_field_count": pending_truth_field_count,
        "pending_truth_fields": sorted(pending_truth_fields),
        "field_metrics": metrics_by_field,
        "field_aggregate": field_aggregate,
        "level_metrics": compute_level_metrics(level_rows) if level_rows else None,
        "image_grade_metrics": (
            compute_image_grade_metrics(image_grade_pairs)
            if image_grade_pairs
            else None
        ),
    })


def compute_image_grade_metrics(
    pairs: Iterable[tuple[Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]], Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把逐案例的逐图档位比对汇总成一份跨案例的混淆矩阵。

    ``missing`` 是真值有档位但模型没给的图——非表现图没有分数，投影不猜档位，
    这类图不该计进准确率，但必须让运营看见有多少张。
    """

    from .design_case_image_grade import IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS, compare_image_grades

    confusion = {
        row: {column: 0 for column in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS}
        for row in IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS
    }
    compared = correct = over = under = 0
    missing = unexpected = 0
    for expected_images, actual_images in pairs:
        result = compare_image_grades(expected_images, actual_images)
        compared += result["compared"]
        correct += result["correct"]
        over += result["over_graded"]
        under += result["under_graded"]
        missing += len(result["missing"])
        unexpected += len(result["unexpected"])
        for truth, row in result["confusion"].items():
            for predicted, count in row.items():
                confusion[truth][predicted] += count
    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-image-grade-metrics-v1",
        "compared": compared,
        "correct": correct,
        "accuracy": _ratio(correct, compared),
        "over_graded": over,
        "under_graded": under,
        "missing": missing,
        "unexpected": unexpected,
        "confusion": confusion,
    }


def _metric_row(item: BaselineRegressionItem) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = _json_object(
        item.result_snapshot_json,
        code="baseline_result_snapshot_invalid",
    )
    status = item.status
    if snapshot.get("manual_required") is True:
        status = "manual_required"
    predicted = snapshot.get("normalized_result")
    runtime = snapshot.get("workflow_runtime")
    return {
        "status": status,
        "workflow_status": runtime.get("status") if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else None,
        "expected_result": _expected_result(item),
        "predicted_result": predicted if isinstance(predicted, Mapping) else None,
    }


def refresh_native_baseline_run(
    run: BaselineRegressionRun,
    *,
    items: Iterable[BaselineRegressionItem] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    metric_items = list(items) if items is not None else run.items
    metrics = compute_truth_metrics(_metric_row(item) for item in metric_items)
    return _apply_native_baseline_metrics(run, metrics)


def _apply_native_baseline_metrics(
    run: BaselineRegressionRun, metrics: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    run.completed = metrics["completed"]
    run.valid_predictions = metrics["valid_predictions"]
    run.failed = metrics["failed"]
    run.metrics_json = canonical_json(metrics)
    if metrics["pending"]:
        run.status = "running"
        run.finished_at = None
    elif metrics["failed"] == run.total:
        run.status = "failed"
        run.finished_at = run.finished_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    elif metrics["failed"] or metrics["manual_required"]:
        run.status = "partial_failed"
        run.finished_at = run.finished_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    else:
        run.status = "completed"
        run.finished_at = run.finished_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    return metrics


def _lock_native_baseline_parent_and_item(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
) -> tuple[BaselineRegressionRun | None, BaselineRegressionItem | None]:
    """Lock the shared parent before one child to serialize counter refreshes."""

    run_id = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.id == item_id
        )
    )
    if run_id is None:
        return None, None
    run = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun)
        .where(BaselineRegressionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if run is None:
        return None, None
    item = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem)
        .where(BaselineRegressionItem.id == item_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    return run, item


class _MetricSnapshotCache:
    """Bounded process-local content cache; never caches mutable status or truth."""

    def __init__(self, max_bytes: int = 32 * 1024 * 1024, max_entries: int = 8192):
        self.max_bytes = max_bytes
        self.max_entries = max_entries
        self._entries: OrderedDict[str, bytes] = OrderedDict()
        self._bytes = 0
        self._lock = Lock()

    def get(self, digest: str) -> bytes | None:
        with self._lock:
            value = self._entries.get(digest)
            if value is not None:
                self._entries.move_to_end(digest)
            return value

    def put(self, digest: str, value: bytes) -> None:
        if len(value) > self.max_bytes or self.max_entries < 1:
            return
        with self._lock:
            previous = self._entries.pop(digest, None)
            if previous is not None:
                self._bytes -= len(previous)
            self._entries[digest] = value
            self._bytes += len(value)
            while self._bytes > self.max_bytes or len(self._entries) > self.max_entries:
                _, removed = self._entries.popitem(last=False)
                self._bytes -= len(removed)


_metric_snapshot_cache = _MetricSnapshotCache()


def _compact_metric_snapshot(raw: str | None) -> bytes:
    snapshot = _json_object(raw, code="baseline_result_snapshot_invalid")
    predicted = snapshot.get("normalized_result")
    runtime = snapshot.get("workflow_runtime")
    return canonical_json({
        "manual_required": snapshot.get("manual_required") is True,
        "normalized_result": predicted if isinstance(predicted, Mapping) else None,
        # 09-30：工作流状态计数随 metrics_json 持久化，列表页不再逐条解析结果快照
        "workflow_status": runtime.get("status") if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else None,
    }).encode("utf-8")


def _native_metric_projection(run_id: int):
    # Hashing TEXT once avoids repeated MySQL JSON parsing and transfers no
    # model evidence on cache hits. The same locking read sees fresh truth/status.
    return select(
        BaselineRegressionItem.id,
        BaselineRegressionItem.status,
        BaselineRegressionItem.expected_result_json,
        BaselineRegressionItem.expected_level,
        func.sha2(func.coalesce(BaselineRegressionItem.result_snapshot_json, ""), 256).label("snapshot_hash"),
    ).where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run_id).order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id)


def _cached_metric_rows(db: Session, rows):
    snapshots = {}
    missing = []
    for row in rows:
        value = _metric_snapshot_cache.get(row.snapshot_hash)
        if value is None:
            missing.append(row.id)
        else:
            snapshots[row.snapshot_hash] = value
    if missing:
        raw_rows = db.execute(select(
            BaselineRegressionItem.id, BaselineRegressionItem.result_snapshot_json,
        ).where(BaselineRegressionItem.id.in_(missing)).with_for_update()).all()
        by_id = {row.id: row for row in rows}
        for raw_row in raw_rows:
            digest = hashlib.sha256((raw_row.result_snapshot_json or "").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            if digest != by_id[raw_row.id].snapshot_hash:
                raise NativeBaselineRegressionError("baseline_result_snapshot_invalid", "冻结快照在汇总期间发生变化")
            value = _compact_metric_snapshot(raw_row.result_snapshot_json)
            snapshots[digest] = value
            _metric_snapshot_cache.put(digest, value)
    for row in rows:
        snapshot = json.loads(snapshots[row.snapshot_hash])
        yield {
            "status": "manual_required" if snapshot["manual_required"] else row.status,
            "workflow_status": snapshot.get("workflow_status"),
            "expected_result": _expected_result(row),
            "predicted_result": snapshot["normalized_result"],
        }


def _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(
    db: Session,
    run: BaselineRegressionRun,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Preserve parent-first current reads while avoiding repeated evidence loads."""
    # Production disables autoflush: include the child just finalized, also on
    # fallback databases. No truth/status is ever sourced from the cache.
    db.flush()
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name != "mysql":
        items = db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run.id,
        ).order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id).with_for_update()).all()
        return refresh_native_baseline_run(run, items=items)
    rows = db.execute(_native_metric_projection(run.id).with_for_update()).all()
    metrics = compute_truth_metrics(_cached_metric_rows(db, rows))
    return _apply_native_baseline_metrics(run, metrics)


def _is_score_only_legacy_chain(production_run: ProductionRun) -> bool:
    """冻结快照里的工作流是否为文档化的旧豆包链（只出分不出等级）。"""
    try:
        snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(production_run)
    except ValueError:
        return False
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    policy = manifest.get("resource_policy") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    return isinstance(policy, Mapping) and policy.get("legacy_semantics") == "documented_php_chain"


def finalize_native_baseline_item(
    db: Session,
    production_run: ProductionRun,
    fact: NativeEvaluationFact,
) -> BaselineRegressionItem:
    if (
        production_run.source_type != "baseline_regression_item"
        or production_run.source_id is None
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_source_invalid",
            "原生运行没有绑定存量回归条目",
        )
    baseline_run, item = _lock_native_baseline_parent_and_item(
        db,
        production_run.source_id,
    )
    if (
        item is None
        or baseline_run is None
        or baseline_run.execution_engine != "native_workflow"
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_source_invalid",
            "原生运行绑定的存量回归条目不存在或执行引擎不匹配",
        )
    if (
        baseline_run.workflow_version_id != fact.workflow_version_id
        or production_run.workflow_version_id != fact.workflow_version_id
        or fact.run_id != production_run.id
        or item.production_run_id not in {None, production_run.id}
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_identity_conflict",
            "存量回归条目与原生工作流版本或运行身份不一致",
        )
    if item.status == "completed" and item.evaluation_id == fact.evaluation_result_id:
        _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_run)
        db.flush()
        return item
    expected = _expected_result(item)
    missing_fields = sorted(
        set(_flatten_fields(expected)) - set(_flatten_fields(fact.normalized_result))
    )
    if missing_fields and _is_score_only_legacy_chain(production_run):
        # 旧豆包灵感图链按老大口径只出 0-100 分写下游，L1-L5 由下游自己做阈值映射；
        # 回归对这条链不再要求 level，只要模型分等字段齐即算完成（137 run 199 实证：
        # 三条模型层全成功却全部 missing_truth_fields:level）。
        missing_fields = [field for field in missing_fields if field != "level"]
    item.production_run_id = production_run.id
    item.job_id = fact.evaluation_job_id
    item.evaluation_id = fact.evaluation_result_id
    evaluation = db.get(EvaluationResult, fact.evaluation_result_id)
    compatibility_snapshot = (
        legacy_result_snapshot(evaluation) if evaluation is not None else {}
    )
    item.result_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
        {
            **compatibility_snapshot,
            "schema_version": "native-baseline-item-result-v1",
            "workflow_version_id": fact.workflow_version_id,
            "production_run_id": fact.run_id,
            "result_hash": fact.result_hash,
            "normalized_result": fact.normalized_result,
            "workflow_node_outputs": fact.node_outputs,
            "workflow_runtime": {
                "run_key": production_run.run_key,
                "status": production_run.status,
                "snapshot_hash": production_run.snapshot_hash,
                "environment": production_run.environment,
            },
        }
    )
    item.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if missing_fields:
        item.status = "failed"
        item.error_message = "missing_truth_fields:" + ",".join(missing_fields)
    else:
        item.status = "completed"
        item.error_message = ""
    _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_run)
    db.flush()
    return item


# 失败节点/错误码 → 运营看得懂的说法。键名对不上的走兜底文案，不让代码词直接上页面。

    "kg_output_field_type_invalid": "模型输出的字段类型不对",
    "structured_output_schema_mismatch": "模型输出不符合提示词冻结的 JSON 结构",

    "model_response_controls_mismatch": "模型返回的控制参数与请求不一致",
    "model_config_snapshot_drift": "该版本冻结的模型已过期（模型管理里的供应商、端点或模型 ID 已变）",
    "model_source_unavailable": "冻结的模型已停用或密钥不可用",
    "model_real_execution_disabled": "管理员已关闭该模型的真实调用授权",
    "model_snapshot_identity_mismatch": "冻结的模型身份与运行参数不一致",
    "workflow_run_canceled": "本轮被人工取消",
    "workflow_run_failed": "工作流执行失败",
}
_FAILURE_HINTS = {
    "kg_output_value_invalid": (
        "这是模型没有守住提示词里的取值规则（例如要求只填 1 个值却给了多个）。"
        "请对照下方「模型实际输出」调整该路线的提示词措辞，不需要改代码。"

    "kg_output_identity_mismatch": "请检查提示词是否要求原样复制传入的素材 ID。",
    "structured_output_schema_mismatch": "请检查提示词节点冻结的 JSON 结构与提示词正文是否一致。",
    "model_config_snapshot_drift": (
        "模型没有被调用、不产生费用。请改选冻结了当前模型的机制版本，"
        "或到工作流管理器重新生成版本后再发起新一轮。"
