    "model_real_execution_disabled": "请联系管理员在模型管理中重新开启真实调用授权。",
}


def _native_failure_detail(
    db: Session,
    production_run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    error_code: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把一次原生子运行的失败整理成运营能直接读的说明。

    以前条目上只留一个错误码（kg_output_value_invalid），运营看不出是哪个节点、
    哪条规则、模型到底输出了什么；排查 run158/159 时只能翻库反推。这里把
    失败节点的人话名、具体原因、被拒的模型输出和处理建议一并冻结进快照。
    """

    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.status == "failed",
        )
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence.desc(), ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
    )
    step_key = str(attempt.step_key) if attempt is not None else None
    code = str(
        (attempt.last_error_code if attempt is not None else None)
        or error_code
        or ""
    ).strip()
    # 只放行我们自己校验器产出的文案（如「color 最多允许 1 个值」）。
    # 供应商/基础设施的原始报错可能带请求细节，按既有守卫不得进入基线快照。
    message = ""
    if attempt is not None and code in _FAILURE_CODE_LABELS and code.startswith("kg_"):
        message = str(attempt.last_error_message or "").strip()[:200]

    stage_label = _FAILURE_STAGE_LABELS.get(step_key or "")
    if stage_label is None and step_key:
        try:
            snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(production_run)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            snapshot = {}
        manifest = (snapshot.get("workflow") or {}).get("manifest") or {}
        for node in manifest.get("nodes") or []:
            if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
                continue
            if str(node.get("node_instance_id") or node.get("key") or "") == step_key:
                stage_label = str(node.get("display_name") or "").strip() or None
                break
    if stage_label is None:
        stage_label = step_key or "工作流"

    rejected_output: Any = None
    if attempt is not None and attempt.checkpoint_json:
        try:
            checkpoint = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            checkpoint = {}
        evidence = (checkpoint.get("failure") or {}).get("evidence") or {}
        if isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
            rejected_output = evidence.get("rejected_model_output")

    reason_label = _FAILURE_CODE_LABELS.get(code, "工作流执行失败")
    summary = f"{stage_label}：{reason_label}"
    if message and message != code:
        summary += f"（{message}）"
    return {
        "schema_version": "native-baseline-item-failure-detail-v1",
        "step_key": step_key,
        "stage_label": stage_label,
        "code": code,
        "message": message,
        "summary": summary,
        "hint": _FAILURE_HINTS.get(code),
        "rejected_model_output": rejected_output,
    }


def _safe_runtime_error_code(production_run: ProductionRun) -> str:
    fallback = (
        "workflow_run_canceled"
        if production_run.status == "canceled"
        else "workflow_run_failed"
    )
    value = str(production_run.error_code or "").strip()
    allowed = frozenset(
        "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789_.:-"
    )
    if not value or len(value) > 128 or any(char not in allowed for char in value):
        return fallback
    return value


def finalize_failed_native_baseline_item(
    db: Session,
    production_run: ProductionRun,
) -> BaselineRegressionItem:
    """Converge one failed native child without persisting provider details."""

    if (
        production_run.source_type != "baseline_regression_item"
        or production_run.source_id is None
        or production_run.status not in {"failed", "canceled"}
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_failure_source_invalid",
            "只有已失败或已取消的原生存量回归子运行可以归并",
        )
    baseline_run, item = _lock_native_baseline_parent_and_item(
        db,
        production_run.source_id,
    )
    if (
        item is None
        or baseline_run is None
        or baseline_run.execution_engine != "native_workflow"
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_source_invalid",
            "原生运行绑定的存量回归条目不存在或执行引擎不匹配",
        )
    if (
        baseline_run.workflow_version_id != production_run.workflow_version_id
        or item.production_run_id not in {None, production_run.id}
    ):
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_identity_conflict",
            "存量回归条目与失败子运行的工作流或运行身份不一致",
        )

    error_code = _safe_runtime_error_code(production_run)
    if item.status == "failed":
        existing = _json_object(
            item.result_snapshot_json,
            code="baseline_result_snapshot_invalid",
        )
        runtime = existing.get("workflow_runtime")
        if (
            existing.get("production_run_id") == production_run.id
            and isinstance(runtime, Mapping)
            and runtime.get("status") == production_run.status
            and runtime.get("error_code") == error_code
        ):
            _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_run)
            db.flush()
            return item
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_failure_conflict",
            "存量回归条目已经归并了不同的失败证据",
        )
    if item.status == "completed":
        raise NativeBaselineRegressionError(
            "native_baseline_failure_conflict",
            "已完成的存量回归条目不能改写为失败",
        )

    item.production_run_id = production_run.id
    item.status = "failed"
    item.error_message = error_code
    failure_detail = _native_failure_detail(
        db, production_run, error_code=error_code
    )
    item.result_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
        {
            "schema_version": "native-baseline-item-failure-v1",
            "workflow_version_id": production_run.workflow_version_id,
            "production_run_id": production_run.id,
            "normalized_result": None,
            "failure": failure_detail,
            "workflow_runtime": {
                "run_key": production_run.run_key,
                "status": production_run.status,
                "error_code": error_code,
                "snapshot_hash": production_run.snapshot_hash,
                "environment": production_run.environment,
            },
        }
    )
    item.finished_at = production_run.finished_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_run)
    db.flush()
    return item


def reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents(
    db: Session,
    *,
    limit: int = 100,
) -> int:
    """Repair running parents whose native children are already all terminal."""

    if limit < 1:
        return 0
    pending_child = select(BaselineRegressionItem.id).where(
        BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == BaselineRegressionRun.id,
        BaselineRegressionItem.status.notin_(("completed", "failed")),
    )
    candidate_ids = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(BaselineRegressionRun.id)
            .where(
                BaselineRegressionRun.execution_engine == "native_workflow",
                BaselineRegressionRun.status == "running",
                ~pending_child.exists(),
            )
            .order_by(BaselineRegressionRun.id.asc())
            .limit(limit)
        )
    )
    repaired = 0
    for run_id in candidate_ids:
        run = db.scalar(
            select(BaselineRegressionRun)
            .where(BaselineRegressionRun.id == run_id)
            .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
        )
        if run is None or run.status != "running":
            continue
        items = db.scalars(
            select(BaselineRegressionItem)
            .where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run.id)
            .order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id)
            .with_for_update()
        ).all()
        if not items or any(
            item.status not in {"completed", "failed"} for item in items
        ):
            continue
        refresh_native_baseline_run(run, items=items)
        repaired += 1
    return repaired


def reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children(
    db: Session,
    *,
    limit: int = 100,
) -> int:
    """Repair pre-hook native children without retrying their model calls.

    Older app versions could persist a terminal ``ProductionRun`` before the
    corresponding stock-regression item was finalized.  Recovery must only
    consume that already-terminal evidence; it must never make a failed model
    request runnable again.
    """

    if limit < 1:
        return 0
    candidate_ids = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ProductionRun.id)
            .join(
                BaselineRegressionItem,
                BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id == ProductionRun.id,
            )
            .join(
                BaselineRegressionRun,
                BaselineRegressionRun.id == BaselineRegressionItem.run_id,
            )
            .where(
                ProductionRun.source_type == "baseline_regression_item",
                ProductionRun.source_id == BaselineRegressionItem.id,
                ProductionRun.status.in_({"failed", "canceled"}),
                BaselineRegressionItem.status == "queued",
                BaselineRegressionRun.execution_engine == "native_workflow",
                BaselineRegressionRun.workflow_version_id
                == ProductionRun.workflow_version_id,
            )
            .order_by(ProductionRun.id.asc())
            .limit(limit)
        )
    )
    reconciled = 0
    for production_run_id in candidate_ids:
        # Completion always locks ProductionRun before BaselineRegressionItem.
        # Locking every joined table in one SELECT lets MySQL choose the reverse
        # order and deadlocks finalizers under parallel workers. Discovery stays
        # lock-free; each worker then claims one run with SKIP LOCKED and follows
        # the same run -> item order as normal completion.
        production_run = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id == production_run_id)
            .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
        )
        if (
            production_run is None
            or production_run.status not in {"failed", "canceled"}
        ):
            continue
        finalize_failed_native_baseline_item(db, production_run)
        reconciled += 1
    return reconciled

===== FILE backend/app/native_source_run_discovery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat


class SourceRunDiscoveryError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceRunEvidence:
    run_id: int
    baseline_set_id: int
    category_key: str
    sequence_no: int
    status: str
    total: int
    completed: int
    valid_predictions: int
    failed: int
    workflow_version_id: int
    workflow_version: str
    workflow_canonical_hash: str
    created_at: datetime
    item_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    failed_item_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    components: tuple[dict[str, object], ...]
    invocation_counts: dict[str, int]


def _safe_scalar(value: Any) -> object | None:
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    return None


def _component_evidence(node: object) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    if not isinstance(node, dict):
        return None
    kind = node.get("kind")
    if kind not in {"prompt", "rule_module", "rule_contract", "level_matcher"}:
        return None
    node_instance_id = _safe_scalar(
        node.get("node_instance_id", node.get("key"))
    )
    if not isinstance(node_instance_id, str) or not node_instance_id:
        return None
    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, dict):
        config = {}
    component_snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
    if not isinstance(component_snapshot, dict):
        component_snapshot = {}

    result: dict[str, object] = {
        "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
        "kind": str(kind),
    }
    if kind == "prompt":
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        if not isinstance(model_snapshot, dict):
            model_snapshot = {}
        runtime_config = model_snapshot.get("runtime_config")
        if not isinstance(runtime_config, dict):
            runtime_config = {}
        safe_values = {
            "prompt_version_id": config.get("prompt_version_id"),
            "component_version": component_snapshot.get("version"),
            "component_snapshot_hash": component_snapshot.get(
                "content_hash", component_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash")
            ),
            "model_registry_id": model_snapshot.get(
                "model_registry_id", config.get("model_registry_id")
            ),
            "provider": model_snapshot.get("provider"),
            "model_id": model_snapshot.get("model_id"),
            "max_tokens": config.get(
                "max_tokens", runtime_config.get("max_tokens")
            ),
            "thinking_level": (
                config.get("thinking_level")
                if config.get("thinking_enabled") is True
                else "none"
            ),
            "thinking_enabled": config.get("thinking_enabled") is True,
            "model_snapshot_hash": model_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash"),
        }
    else:
        safe_values = {
            "rule_module_version_id": config.get("rule_module_version_id"),
            "module_type_key": config.get("module_type_key"),
            "component_version": component_snapshot.get("version"),
            "component_snapshot_hash": component_snapshot.get(
                "content_hash", component_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash")
            ),
        }
    for key, value in safe_values.items():
        safe_value = _safe_scalar(value)
        if safe_value is not None:
            result[key] = safe_value
    return result


def _usable_manifest(
    version: WorkflowVersion, *, category_key: str
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if version.version.lower().startswith(("candidate-", "candidate_")):
        return None
    try:
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if manifest.get("category_key") != category_key:
        return None
    run_modes = manifest.get("run_modes")
    if not isinstance(run_modes, list) or "stock_regression" not in run_modes:
        return None
    return manifest


def _frozen_identity_matches(
    run: BaselineRegressionRun,
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    manifest: dict[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    try:
        execution = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)
        frozen_workflow = execution["workflow_snapshot"]["workflow"]
    except (KeyError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return False
    if execution.get("model_execution_mode") != "real":
        return False
    if not isinstance(frozen_workflow, dict):
        return False
    if frozen_workflow.get("workflow_version_id") != version.id:
        return False
    if frozen_workflow.get("version") != version.version:
        return False
    if frozen_workflow.get("canonical_hash") != version.canonical_hash:
        return False
    frozen_manifest = frozen_workflow.get("manifest")
    if not isinstance(frozen_manifest, dict):
        return False
    current_components = tuple(
        evidence
        for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if (evidence := _component_evidence(node)) is not None
    )
    frozen_components = tuple(
        evidence
        for node in frozen_manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if (evidence := _component_evidence(node)) is not None
    )
    return current_components == frozen_components


def discover_latest_usable_run(
    db: Session, *, category_key: str, minimum_items: int = 1
) -> SourceRunEvidence:
    if (
        not isinstance(minimum_items, int)
        or isinstance(minimum_items, bool)
        or minimum_items < 1
    ):
        raise SourceRunDiscoveryError(
            "source_run_minimum_invalid", "来源 run 最少条数必须是正整数"
        )
    candidates = db.execute(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun, WorkflowVersion)
        .join(
            WorkflowVersion,
            WorkflowVersion.id == BaselineRegressionRun.workflow_version_id,
        )
        .join(BaselineSet, BaselineSet.id == BaselineRegressionRun.baseline_set_id)
        .where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.category_key == category_key,
            BaselineSet.category_key == category_key,
            BaselineRegressionRun.execution_engine == "native_workflow",
            BaselineRegressionRun.status.in_(("completed", "partial_failed")),
            BaselineRegressionRun.valid_predictions > 0,
            BaselineRegressionRun.total >= minimum_items,
            WorkflowVersion.status == "active",
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3",
        )
        .order_by(
            BaselineRegressionRun.created_at.desc(),
            BaselineRegressionRun.id.desc(),
        )
    ).all()
    for run, version in candidates:
        manifest = _usable_manifest(version, category_key=category_key)
        if manifest is None or not _frozen_identity_matches(run, version, manifest):
            continue
        items = db.scalars(
            select(BaselineRegressionItem)
            .where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run.id)
            .order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id)
        ).all()
        if len(items) < minimum_items:
            continue
        components = tuple(
            evidence
            for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
            if (evidence := _component_evidence(node)) is not None
        )
        invocation_counts = {
            str(status): int(count)
            for status, count in db.execute(
                select(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.status,
                    func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id),
                )
                .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.baseline_run_id == run.id)
                .group_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.status)
                .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.status)
            ).all()
        }
        if invocation_counts.get("succeeded", 0) < 1:
            continue
        return SourceRunEvidence(
            run_id=run.id,
            baseline_set_id=run.baseline_set_id,
            category_key=run.category_key,
            sequence_no=run.sequence_no,
            status=run.status,
            total=run.total,
            completed=run.completed,
            valid_predictions=run.valid_predictions,
            failed=run.failed,
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            workflow_version=version.version,
            workflow_canonical_hash=version.canonical_hash,
            created_at=run.created_at,
            item_ids=tuple(item.baseline_set_item_id for item in items),
            failed_item_ids=tuple(
                item.baseline_set_item_id
                for item in items
                if item.status == "failed"
            ),
            components=components,
            invocation_counts=invocation_counts,
        )
    raise SourceRunDiscoveryError(
        "source_run_not_found",
        f"类目 {category_key} 没有可用于验收的原生存量回归来源",
    )


def redacted_source_run_payload(evidence: SourceRunEvidence) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "run_id": evidence.run_id,
        "baseline_set_id": evidence.baseline_set_id,
        "category_key": evidence.category_key,
        "sequence_no": evidence.sequence_no,
        "status": evidence.status,
        "total": evidence.total,
        "completed": evidence.completed,
        "valid_predictions": evidence.valid_predictions,
        "failed": evidence.failed,
        "workflow_version_id": evidence.workflow_version_id,
        "workflow_version": evidence.workflow_version,
        "workflow_canonical_hash": evidence.workflow_canonical_hash,
        "created_at": platform_isoformat(evidence.created_at),
        "item_ids": list(evidence.item_ids),
        "failed_item_ids": list(evidence.failed_item_ids),
        "components": [dict(component) for component in evidence.components],
        "invocation_counts": dict(evidence.invocation_counts),
    }

===== FILE backend/app/native_workflow_migration.py =====
