class NativeWorkflowMigrationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquivalenceReport:
    ok: bool
    compared_fields: tuple[str, ...]
    mismatches: tuple[str, ...]
    legacy_hash: str
    native_hash: str



logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

def _knowledge_graph_prompt_sources() -> dict[str, str]:
    path = Path(__file__).with_name("knowledge_graph_prompt_assets.json")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "knowledge_graph_prompt_source_invalid",
            "知识图谱四路提示词源文件缺失或损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "knowledge_graph_prompt_source_invalid",
            "知识图谱四路提示词源文件必须是对象",
        )
    prompts = payload.get("prompts")
    expected = {str(route["route_key"]) for route in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES}
    if (
        payload.get("schema_version") != "knowledge-graph-prompt-source-v1"
        or not isinstance(prompts, Mapping)
        or set(prompts) != expected
        or any(
            not isinstance(prompts[key], str) or not prompts[key].strip()
            for key in expected
        )
    ):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "knowledge_graph_prompt_source_invalid",
            "知识图谱四路提示词源文件与路由合同不一致",
        )
    return {key: str(prompts[key]).strip() for key in sorted(expected)}


def knowledge_graph_prompt_sources() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Return a detached copy of the frozen four-route prompt source.

    Release tooling needs the same authoritative source as the native workflow
    migration.  Keeping the parser private while exposing a copy prevents
    callers from mutating the module-owned mapping or creating a second loader.
    """

    return deepcopy(_knowledge_graph_prompt_sources())


def resolve_legacy_native_workflow(
    db: Session,
    *,
    legacy_revision_id: int,
    category_key: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    """Resolve a historical revision only through its frozen native mapping."""

    revision = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, legacy_revision_id)
    mappings = db.scalars(
        select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
            LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id
            == legacy_revision_id
        )
    ).all()
    if (
        revision is None
        or revision.category_key != category_key
        or len(mappings) != 1
    ):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_historical_rerun_read_only",
            "该历史评测版本没有唯一的原生工作流映射，只能查看旧结果",
        )
    mapping = mappings[0]
    workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
    if workflow is None:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_historical_rerun_read_only",
            "该历史评测版本映射的原生工作流已不可用，只能查看旧结果",
        )
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
    policy = manifest.get("resource_policy")
    identity = (
        policy.get("migration_identity")
        if isinstance(policy, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    try:
        compatibility = json.loads(mapping.compatibility_report_json or "{}")
        validation = json.loads(workflow.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_historical_rerun_read_only",
            "该历史评测版本的原生兼容证据损坏，只能查看旧结果",
        ) from exc
    if (
        workflow.status != "active"
        or workflow.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3"
        or manifest.get("category_key") != category_key
        or "stock_regression" not in (manifest.get("run_modes") or [])
        or not isinstance(identity, Mapping)
        or identity.get("legacy_revision_id") != legacy_revision_id
        or not isinstance(compatibility, Mapping)
        or compatibility.get("compatible") is not True
        or not isinstance(validation, Mapping)
        or validation.get("ok") is not True
    ):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_historical_rerun_read_only",
            "该历史评测版本未通过原生工作流兼容门禁，只能查看旧结果",
        )
    return workflow


def _definition(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
    *,
    actor: str,
    display_name: str | None = None,
) -> WorkflowDefinition:
    workflow_key = f"{category_key}.evaluation"
    generated_name = f"{category_key} 评测工作流"
    desired_name = display_name or generated_name
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        return create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=workflow_key,
            name=desired_name,
            description="由存量评测机制幂等迁移为原生工作流。",
            owner="operations",
            allowed_categories=[category_key],
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if display_name and row.name == generated_name:
        row.name = display_name
    try:
        allowed = json.loads(row.allowed_categories_json or "[]")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_definition_invalid",
            "工作流定义的类目范围损坏",
        ) from exc
    if allowed != [category_key]:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_definition_conflict",
            "已有工作流定义的类目范围与迁移目标不一致",
        )
    return row


def _model_for_category(
    db: Session, profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile
) -> ModelRegistryEntry:
    candidates = db.scalars(
        select(ModelRegistryEntry)
        .where(
            ModelRegistryEntry.role == "main",
            ModelRegistryEntry.active.is_(True),
        )
        .order_by(ModelRegistryEntry.id)
    ).all()
    if profile.model_config_id is not None:
        matched = [
            row
            for row in candidates
            if row.source_model_config_id == profile.model_config_id
        ]
        if len(matched) == 1:
            return matched[0]
    if len(candidates) != 1:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_model_ambiguous",
            "类目没有唯一可冻结的主模型配置",
        )
    return candidates[0]


def _prompt_for_revision(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    asset_key: str,
    stage: str,
    version: Any,
) -> PromptVersion:
    if not isinstance(version, str) or not version.strip():
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_prompt_binding_missing",
            f"历史机制缺少提示词 {stage} 版本绑定",
        )
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
        )
    )
    if asset is None:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_prompt_asset_missing",
            f"类目缺少提示词节点 {asset_key}",
        )
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .join(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == PromptVersion.id,
        )
        .where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            PromptVersion.category_key == category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == stage,
            PromptVersion.version == version,
            PromptVersion.status != "archived",
        )
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id)
    ).all()
    if len(rows) != 1:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_prompt_binding_ambiguous",
            f"历史机制的提示词 {stage} 版本无法唯一解析：{version}",
        )
    return rows[0]


def _node(
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    display_name: str,
    config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "config": deepcopy(dict(config)),
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 1,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": [
            "request_id",
            "trace_id",
            "legacy_revision_id",
        ],
    }


def _knowledge_graph_prompt_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    route: Mapping[str, Any],
    system_prompt: str,
    actor: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    route_key = str(route["route_key"])
    asset_key = f"kg_{route_key}_prompt"
    stage = str(route["stage"])
    desired = {
        "stage": stage,
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": "",
        "rubric_version": "jiaxin-knowledge-graph-v1",
    }
    desired_hash = canonical_component_hash(desired)
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )
    if asset is None:
        asset = create_component_asset(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key=asset_key,
            kind="prompt",
            display_name=str(route["display_name"]),
            prompt_stage=stage,
            description=(
                "嘉欣知识图谱字段版本 v1 对应的独立提示词节点；"
                "后续版本由运营在工作流管理器中维护。"
            ),
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if asset.kind != "prompt":
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "knowledge_graph_prompt_asset_conflict",
            f"组件 {asset_key} 已存在但不是提示词节点",
        )
    versions = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion)
        .join(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == PromptVersion.id,
        )
        .where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            PromptVersion.status != "archived",
        )
        .order_by(PromptVersion.id)
    ).all()
    for version in versions:
        content = {
            "stage": version.stage,
            "system_prompt": version.system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": version.user_prompt,
            "rubric_version": version.rubric_version,
        }
        if canonical_component_hash(content) == desired_hash:
            return version
    working = get_component_working_copy(db, asset.id)
    try:
        current = json.loads(working.content_json)
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        current = None
    if current != desired:
        if versions:
            logger.warning(
                "知识图谱提示词源 %s 已更新，保留已冻结版本 %s；"
                "新正文只能通过新提示词/工作流候选发布",
                asset_key,
                versions[0].version,
            )
            return versions[0]
        save_component_working_copy(
            db,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
            content=desired,
            updated_by=actor,
        )
    reference = generate_component_version(
        db,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, reference.id)
    assert prompt is not None
    return prompt


def _knowledge_graph_manifest(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    prompts: Mapping[str, PromptVersion],
    model: ModelRegistryEntry,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    max_tokens = max(128, min(int(model.max_tokens), 65536))
    thinking_level = "none"
    route_keys = [str(route["route_key"]) for route in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES]
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取知识图谱待打标素材",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.knowledge_graph.input"},
        ),
        _node(
            "route_selector",
            "branch",
            "按来源与整体/单体选择路线",
            {
                "branch_key": "knowledge_graph_route",
                "branches": route_keys,
                "mode": "exclusive",
            },
        ),
    ]
    nodes[0]["error_policy"] = {"kind": "manual_review"}
    nodes[1]["error_policy"] = {"kind": "manual_review"}
    for route in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES:
        route_key = str(route["route_key"])
        asset_scope = str(route["asset_scope"])
        contract_key = (
            f"kg.{category_key}.{route['site_scope']}.{asset_scope}.v1"
        )
        nodes.extend(
            [
                _node(
                    f"prompt_{route_key}",
                    "prompt",
                    str(route["display_name"]),
                    {
                        "asset_key": f"kg_{route_key}_prompt",
                        "prompt_version_id": prompts[route_key].id,
                        "model_registry_id": model.id,
                        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                        "thinking_level": thinking_level,
                        "thinking_enabled": False,
                        "output_contract_key": contract_key,
                    },
                ),
                _node(
                    f"project_{route_key}",
                    "downstream_projection",
                    (
                        "生成国内知识图谱写入载荷"
                        if route["site_scope"] == "domestic"
                        else "生成海外知识图谱写入载荷"
                    ),
                    {
                        "connector_key": (
                            f"logical.{route['logical_target']}.output"
                        ),
                        "logical_target": route["logical_target"],
                        "field_contract_key": contract_key,
                    },
                ),
            ]
        )
    nodes.append(
        _node(
            "route_join",
            "join",
            "汇合本次实际执行路线",
            {"join_for": "knowledge_graph_route", "mode": "all"},
        )
    )
    edges: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"from": "upstream", "to": "route_selector"}
    ]
    for route in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES:
        route_key = str(route["route_key"])
        edges.extend(
            [
                {
                    "from": "route_selector",
                    "to": f"prompt_{route_key}",
                    "branch": route_key,
                    "condition": {
                        "all": [
                            {
                                "op": "eq",
                                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                                "value": route["site_scope"],
                            },
                            {
                                "op": "eq",
                                "path": "steps.upstream.output.is_single",
                                "value": route["is_single"],
                            },
                        ]
                    },
                },
                {
                    "from": f"prompt_{route_key}",
                    "to": f"project_{route_key}",
                },
                {"from": f"project_{route_key}", "to": "route_join"},
            ]
        )
    layout = {
        "upstream": {"x": 64, "y": 372},
        "route_selector": {"x": 344, "y": 372},
        "route_join": {"x": 1216, "y": 372},
    }
    for index, route in enumerate(_KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES):
        route_key = str(route["route_key"])
        y = 72 + index * 200
        layout[f"prompt_{route_key}"] = {"x": 648, "y": y}
        layout[f"project_{route_key}"] = {"x": 944, "y": y}
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "run_modes": [
            "labeling",
            "evaluation",
            "prompt_regression",
            "stock_regression",
        ],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
        "layout": layout,
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {
            "max_parallel": 4,
            "knowledge_graph_source": {
                "schema_version": "knowledge-graph-prompt-source-v1",
                "source_document": "QG53mjyd80RyjgjvHeRPwzepV6zbX04v",
                "field_contract_version": "v1",
                "physical_targets_ready": False,
            },
        },
    }


def ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
    db: Session,
    actor: str,
    *,
    category_key: str = "model_3d_su",
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    """Materialize the deterministic four-route knowledge-graph workflow."""

    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key,
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == "active",
        )
    )
    if profile is None:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "knowledge_graph_category_missing",
            "知识图谱类目不存在或已停用，无法建立四路工作流",
            status_code=404,
        )
    definition = _definition(
        db,
        category_key,
        actor=actor,
        display_name=(
            "知识图谱打标评测工作流"
            if category_key == "knowledge_graph"
            else None
        ),
    )
    model = _model_for_category(db, profile)
    sources = _knowledge_graph_prompt_sources()
    prompts = {
        str(route["route_key"]): _knowledge_graph_prompt_version(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            route=route,
            system_prompt=sources[str(route["route_key"])],
            actor=actor,
        )
        for route in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES
    }
    frozen = freeze_component_references(
        db,
        _knowledge_graph_manifest(
            category_key=category_key,
            prompts=prompts,
            model=model,
        ),
    )
    assert_native_workflow_executable(frozen)
    # v2（2026-09-02）：海外 is_single 编码改为 整体=1/单体=2，分支条件、字段合同快照
    # 与四路提示词随之变化。已存的 kg-native-v1 原样保留（版本列表会标「路线编码已过期」
    # 并禁止发起），新版本按当前模板生成，运营改选即可。
    version_key = "kg-native-v2"
    workflow = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == version_key,
        )
    )
    if workflow is None:
        workflow = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version=version_key,
            manifest=frozen,
            created_by=actor,
        )
    elif _comparable_manifest(
        persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
    ) != _comparable_manifest(frozen):
        # 与灵感图历史投影同款降级：漂移只告警，不阻断启动（模型 max_tokens
        # 调整会让所有投影漂移，这里是同一颗雷的第二个引信——2026-08-28 部署
        # 因它再次失败并回滚失败）。已存版本保持原样。
        logger.warning(
            "知识图谱投影 %s 与已存版本内容不同，保留已存版本并跳过刷新",
            version_key,
        )
        return workflow
    workflow.status = "active"
    workflow.validation_report_json = canonical_json(
        {
            "ok": True,
            "errors": [],
            "knowledge_graph_readiness": {
                "schema_version": "knowledge-graph-readiness-v1",
                "prompt_routes": list(prompts),
                "logical_targets": [
                    "knowledge_graph_domestic",
                    "knowledge_graph_overseas",
                ],
                "physical_targets_ready": False,
            },
        }
    )
    db.flush()
    return workflow


def _native_manifest(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    revision: CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion,
    model: ModelRegistryEntry,
    module_versions: Mapping[str, int],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    max_tokens = max(128, min(int(model.max_tokens), 65536))
    thinking_level = "none"
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            "读取冻结评测输入",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.input"},
        ),
        _node(
            "prompt_a",
            "prompt",
            "提示词 A",
            {
                "asset_key": "prompt_a",
                "prompt_version_id": prompt_a.id,
                "model_registry_id": model.id,
                "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                "thinking_level": thinking_level,
                "thinking_enabled": False,
            },
        ),
    ]
    definitions = {item.key: item for item in module_type_catalog()}
    for index, module_type_key in enumerate(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS, start=1):
        if module_type_key == "media_adjustment":
            nodes.append(
                _node(
                    "prompt_b",
                    "prompt",
                    "提示词 B",
                    {
                        "asset_key": "prompt_b",
                        "prompt_version_id": prompt_b.id,
                        "model_registry_id": model.id,
                        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                        "thinking_level": thinking_level,
                        "thinking_enabled": False,
                    },
                )
            )
        previous_id = str(nodes[-1]["node_instance_id"])
        node_id = f"{module_type_key}_{index}"
        nodes.append(
            _node(
                node_id,
                "rule_module",
                definitions[module_type_key].display_name,
                {
                    "asset_key": module_type_key,
                    "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                    "rule_module_version_id": module_versions[module_type_key],
                    "input_bindings": {"legacy_input": f"{previous_id}.output"},
                    "scope": f"legacy_revision_{revision.id}",
                },
            )
        )
    nodes.append(
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            "输出评测结果",
            {"connector_key": f"logical.{category_key}.output"},
        )
    )
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "evaluation",
        "run_modes": [
            "labeling",
            "evaluation",
            "prompt_regression",
            "stock_regression",
        ],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {
                "from": source["node_instance_id"],
                "to": target["node_instance_id"],
            }
            for source, target in zip(nodes, nodes[1:])
        ],
        "layout": {
            node["node_instance_id"]: {"x": 64 + index * 260, "y": 96}
            for index, node in enumerate(nodes)
        },
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {
            "max_parallel": 1,
            "migration_identity": {
                "schema_version": "native-category-workflow-migration-v1",
                "legacy_revision_id": revision.id,
                "legacy_revision": revision.revision,
            },
        },
    }


def _projection_snapshot(bundle: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    slices = split_legacy_revision(bundle)
    workflow_fields = slices.workflow_fields
    return {
        "schema_version": "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v2",
        "available": True,
        "workflow_fields": workflow_fields,
        "workflow_fields_hash": hash_manifest(workflow_fields),
        "presence": sorted(slices.presence),
        "presence_hash": hash_manifest({"paths": sorted(slices.presence)}),
    }


def _readiness_report(
    *,
    revision: CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    legacy_bundle: Mapping[str, Any],
    frozen_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    compatible: bool,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    prompt_hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    model_hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    module_hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    for node in frozen_manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
        component = config.get("component_snapshot")
        component = component if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
            prompt_hashes[node_id] = str(component.get("content_hash") or "")
            model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
            model_snapshot = (
                model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
            )
            model_hashes[node_id] = str(model_snapshot.get("snapshot_hash") or "")
        elif node.get("kind") == "rule_module":
            module_hashes[node_id] = str(component.get("content_hash") or "")
    hashes_complete = all(
        len(value) == 64
        for value in (*prompt_hashes.values(), *model_hashes.values(), *module_hashes.values())
    )
    return {
        "schema_version": "native-workflow-readiness-v1",
        "compatible": bool(compatible and hashes_complete),
        "legacy_revision_id": revision.id,
        "legacy_revision": revision.revision,
        "legacy_bundle_hash": hash_manifest(dict(legacy_bundle)),
        "prompt_hashes": prompt_hashes,
        "model_hashes": model_hashes,
        "module_hashes": module_hashes,
        "branch_modes_explicit": all(
            isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping)
            and node["config"].get("mode") in {"parallel", "exclusive"}
            for node in frozen_manifest.get("nodes", [])
            if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "branch"
        ),
    }


# 调度参数不属于评测机制的定义：并发、重试次数、超时只影响怎么跑，不影响跑出
# 什么结果。它们却被冻结进了历史工作流快照，于是运营在模型管理里调一次并发，
# 下次重启时这里就会判定「内容与迁移结果不同」并让整个服务起不来
# （2026-08-28：并发 8→3 后测试服重启失败、回滚也失败，容器陷入重启循环）。
# 比较前把这些字段剥掉：真正的漂移（提示词、规则、模型身份）依然会被发现。
_SCHEDULING_ONLY_FIELDS = ("max_concurrency", "max_retries", "timeout_seconds")


def _comparable_manifest(manifest: Any) -> str:
    """Canonical form of a manifest with scheduling-only knobs stripped."""

    def strip(value: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            return {
                key: strip(item)
                for key, item in value.items()
                if key not in _SCHEDULING_ONLY_FIELDS
            }
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [strip(item) for item in value]
        return value

    return canonical_json(strip(manifest))


def ensure_native_category_workflows(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> list[WorkflowVersion]:
    """Create one executable, immutable workflow for every legacy revision."""

    if category_key not in _SUPPORTED_CATEGORIES:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_category_unsupported",
            "当前只迁移灵感图和 3D&SU 的旧权威机制",
            status_code=422,
        )
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key,
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == "active",
        )
    )
    if profile is None:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_workflow_category_missing",
            "待迁移类目不存在或已停用",
            status_code=404,
        )
    definition = _definition(db, category_key, actor=actor)
    model = _model_for_category(db, profile)
    component_result = migrate_category_component_history(
        db, category_key, actor=actor
    )
    revisions = db.scalars(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision)
        .where(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key)
        .order_by(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision,
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id,
        )
    ).all()
    migrated: list[WorkflowVersion] = []
    for revision in revisions:
        legacy_bundle = revision_bundle(revision)
        contract = legacy_bundle.get("contract")
        bindings = (
            contract.get("prompt_bindings")
            if isinstance(contract, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if not isinstance(bindings, Mapping):
            raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
                "native_workflow_prompt_binding_missing",
                f"历史机制 r{revision.revision} 缺少提示词绑定",
            )
        prompt_a = _prompt_for_revision(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key="prompt_a",
            stage="A",
            version=bindings.get("call_a_version"),
        )
        prompt_b = _prompt_for_revision(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            asset_key="prompt_b",
            stage="B",
            version=bindings.get("call_b_version"),
        )
        projection_report = component_result.compatibility_reports[revision.id]
        if not projection_report.compatible:
            if revision.status != "active":
                # 一个拆分不完整的候选修订不能阻断其它修订的原生工作流生成；
                # 它本身没有唯一原生映射，运行时会被 fail-closed 拒绝，只告警跳过。
                logger.warning(
                    "历史机制 r%s(id=%s) 模块拆分不完整，跳过原生工作流生成：%s",
                    revision.revision,
                    revision.id,
                    ",".join(projection_report.different_paths[:5]),
                )
                continue
            raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
                "native_workflow_projection_incompatible",
                f"历史机制 r{revision.revision} 的模块拆分不完整",
            )
        frozen = freeze_component_references(
            db,
            _native_manifest(
                category_key=category_key,
                revision=revision,
                prompt_a=prompt_a,
                prompt_b=prompt_b,
                model=model,
                module_versions=component_result.revision_slices[revision.id],
            ),
        )
        resource_policy = frozen.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
        resource_policy["legacy_projection_snapshot"] = _projection_snapshot(
            legacy_bundle
        )
        readiness = _readiness_report(
            revision=revision,
            legacy_bundle=legacy_bundle,
            frozen_manifest=frozen,
            compatible=projection_report.compatible,
        )
        if not readiness["compatible"]:
            raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
                "native_workflow_snapshot_incomplete",
                f"历史机制 r{revision.revision} 缺少冻结组件或模型哈希",
            )
        resource_policy["native_readiness"] = readiness
        assert_native_workflow_executable(frozen)
        version_key = f"native-r{revision.revision}"
        workflow = db.scalar(
            select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
                WorkflowVersion.version == version_key,
            )
        )
        if workflow is None:
            workflow = create_workflow_version(
                db,
                definition=definition,
                version=version_key,
                manifest=frozen,
                created_by=actor,
            )
        elif _comparable_manifest(
            persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
        ) != _comparable_manifest(frozen):
            # 漂移只告警，不再让服务起不来。这段自检曾两次把测试服打死：
            # 改并发（已进豁免清单）和改 max_tokens（影响输出，不能豁免）都会
            # 让投影结果与已存版本不同——但历史投影是只读的兼容层，它落后于
            # 当前模型配置不该阻断整个平台启动。已冻结的历史版本保持原样，
            # 新的运行请用工作流管理器生成的新版本。
            logger.warning(
                "历史机制投影 %s 与已存版本内容不同，保留已存版本并跳过刷新；"
                "如需按当前配置运行请生成新的工作流版本",
                version_key,
            )
            continue
        workflow.status = "active"
        workflow.validation_report_json = canonical_json(
            {"ok": True, "errors": [], "native_readiness": readiness}
        )
        mapping = db.scalar(
            select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id == revision.id
            )
        )
        mapping_report = {
            "schema_version": "native-workflow-migration-compatibility-v1",
            "compatible": True,
            "legacy_projection": asdict(projection_report),
            "native_readiness": readiness,
        }
        if mapping is None:
            mapping = LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping(
                legacy_revision_id=revision.id,
                workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                module_versions_json=canonical_json(
                    component_result.revision_slices[revision.id]
                ),
                compatibility_report_json=canonical_json(mapping_report),
                validator_version="native-workflow-equivalence-v1",
                migration_batch_key=f"native-workflow-v1:{category_key}",
            )
            db.add(mapping)
        else:
            if mapping.workflow_version_id != workflow.id:
                previous = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
                if previous is not None and not (
                    previous.version.startswith("legacy-r")
                    or previous.version.startswith("native-r")
                ):
                    raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
                        "native_workflow_mapping_conflict",
                        "历史机制已绑定运营维护的其他工作流，拒绝自动覆盖",
                    )
                if previous is not None and previous.status == "active":
                    previous.status = "deprecated"
                mapping.workflow_version_id = workflow.id
            mapping.module_versions_json = canonical_json(
                component_result.revision_slices[revision.id]
            )
            mapping.compatibility_report_json = canonical_json(mapping_report)
            mapping.validator_version = "native-workflow-equivalence-v1"
            mapping.migration_batch_key = f"native-workflow-v1:{category_key}"
        migrated.append(workflow)
    db.flush()
    return migrated


def _result_scoring(
    v3_context: Mapping[str, Any],
    precheck: Mapping[str, Any],
    dimension_output: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=dict(v3_context),
        precheck=dict(precheck),
        dimension_output=dict(dimension_output),
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=dict(precheck))
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    return scoring


def _node_outputs(
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    precheck: Mapping[str, Any],
    dimension_output: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    outputs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        business: dict[str, Any] = {}
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt" and config.get("asset_key") == "prompt_a":
            business = {"precheck": deepcopy(dict(precheck))}
        elif node.get("kind") == "prompt" and config.get("asset_key") == "prompt_b":
            business = {"dimension_output": deepcopy(dict(dimension_output))}
        outputs[node_id] = {
            "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
            "node_instance_id": node_id,
            "node_kind": node.get("kind"),
            "status": "succeeded",
            "output": business,
            "evidence": {},
        }
    return outputs


def _comparison_view(
    scoring: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    precheck: Mapping[str, Any],
    correction_result: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "precheck": deepcopy(dict(precheck)),
        "redline": {
            "hard_reject": scoring.get("hard_reject"),
            "hit_rules": deepcopy(scoring.get("hit_rules") or []),
        },
        "track": {
            "track_key": scoring.get("track_key"),
            "track_adjustment": deepcopy(scoring.get("track_adjustment")),
        },
        "media_adjustment": {
            "enabled": scoring.get("media_penalty_enabled"),
            "media_key": scoring.get("media_key"),
            "penalty": scoring.get("media_penalty"),
        },
        "quality_cap_exception": {
            "caps": deepcopy(scoring.get("caps") or []),
            "hard_defect_action": deepcopy(scoring.get("hard_defect_action")),
            "hard_defect_penalty": scoring.get("hard_defect_penalty"),
        },
        "dimension_scores": {
            "mode": scoring.get("dimension_scoring_mode"),
            "output": deepcopy(scoring.get("dimension_deduction_output")),
            "evidence": deepcopy(scoring.get("dimension_evidence")),
        },
        "anchor": {
            "foundation_sha256": scoring.get("foundation_sha256"),
            "before_rules": deepcopy(scoring.get("foundation_before_rules")),
            "after_rules": deepcopy(scoring.get("foundation_after_rules")),
        },
        "score": scoring.get("score"),
        "level": scoring.get("level"),
        "confidence": scoring.get("confidence"),
        "needs_review": scoring.get("needs_review"),
        "key_fields": deepcopy(precheck.get("production_fields", {})),
        "correction_result": deepcopy(dict(correction_result or {})),
    }


def _different_paths(left: Any, right: Any, path: str = "") -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(left, Mapping) and isinstance(right, Mapping):
        paths: list[str] = []
        for key in sorted(set(left) | set(right)):
            child = f"{path}.{key}" if path else str(key)
            if key not in left or key not in right:
                paths.append(child)
            else:
                paths.extend(_different_paths(left[key], right[key], child))
        return paths
    if isinstance(left, list) and isinstance(right, list):
        if len(left) != len(right):
            return [path]
        paths = []
        for index, (left_item, right_item) in enumerate(zip(left, right, strict=True)):
            paths.extend(
                _different_paths(left_item, right_item, f"{path}[{index}]")
            )
        return paths
    return [] if left == right else [path]


def compare_legacy_and_native_fixture(
    category_key: str,
    legacy_fixture: Mapping[str, Any],
    workflow_version: WorkflowVersion,
) -> EquivalenceReport:
    """Compare frozen A/B outputs through the old reducer and native reducer."""

    if category_key not in _SUPPORTED_CATEGORIES:
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_equivalence_category_unsupported",
            "等价门禁只适用于灵感图和 3D&SU",
            status_code=422,
        )
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow_version)
    if (
        workflow_version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3"
        or manifest.get("category_key") != category_key
    ):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_equivalence_workflow_mismatch",
            "待比较工作流与类目不一致",
        )
    context = legacy_fixture.get("v3_context")
    precheck = legacy_fixture.get("precheck")
    dimension_output = legacy_fixture.get("dimension_output")
    if not all(
        isinstance(value, Mapping)
        for value in (context, precheck, dimension_output)
    ):
        raise NativeWorkflowMigrationError(
            "native_equivalence_fixture_invalid",
            "成对等价夹具缺少旧机制、调用 A 或调用 B 的结构化输出",
            status_code=422,
        )
    legacy_scoring = _result_scoring(context, precheck, dimension_output)
    native_scoring = reduce_native_workflow_result(
        manifest,
        _node_outputs(
            manifest,
            precheck=precheck,
            dimension_output=dimension_output,
        ),
        model_execution_mode="simulated",
    )
    corrected_dimension = legacy_fixture.get("corrected_dimension_output")
    corrected_precheck = legacy_fixture.get("corrected_precheck", precheck)
    legacy_correction: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    native_correction: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    if isinstance(corrected_dimension, Mapping) and isinstance(
        corrected_precheck, Mapping
    ):
        legacy_correction = _result_scoring(
            context, corrected_precheck, corrected_dimension
        )
        native_correction = reduce_native_workflow_result(
            manifest,
            _node_outputs(
                manifest,
                precheck=corrected_precheck,
                dimension_output=corrected_dimension,
            ),
            model_execution_mode="simulated",
        )
    legacy_view = _comparison_view(
        legacy_scoring,
        precheck=precheck,
        correction_result=legacy_correction,
    )
    native_view = _comparison_view(
        native_scoring,
        precheck=precheck,
        correction_result=native_correction,
    )
    mismatches = tuple(_different_paths(legacy_view, native_view))
    return EquivalenceReport(
        ok=not mismatches,
        compared_fields=_COMPARE_FIELDS,
        mismatches=mismatches,
        legacy_hash=hash_manifest(legacy_view),
        native_hash=hash_manifest(native_view),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/new_server_acceptance.py =====
"""Offline four-hour evidence checks, not a daily throughput extrapolator.

Input must be a complete, source-backed inventory for the scoped workload plus
aggregate 15-second samples from ALL workers. No DB access, notifications or
capacity enablement happens here. Source provenance remains a human audit gate.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import math


def timestamp(value):
    result = datetime.fromisoformat(value) if isinstance(value, str) else value
    if not isinstance(result, datetime) or result.tzinfo is None or result.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError('timestamps must include a timezone')
    return result.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def number(value):
    if type(value) not in {int, float} or not math.isfinite(value) or value < 0:
        raise ValueError('metrics must be finite nonnegative numbers')
    return value


def assess_window(*, start, end, samples, items, events, receipts=None,
                  evidence_kind, inventory_complete=False):
    receipts = [] if receipts is None else receipts  # accepted for old file compatibility, unused
    start, end = timestamp(start), timestamp(end)
    duration = (end - start).total_seconds()
    if not 0 < duration <= 31 * 86400:
        raise ValueError('window must be positive and at most 31 days')
    if any(not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 200_000
           for rows in (samples, items, events, receipts)):
        raise ValueError('evidence collections must be lists bounded by 200000 rows')
    blockers = set()
    if duration < 4 * 3600: blockers.add('window_short')
    if inventory_complete is not True: blockers.add('inventory_incomplete')
    times = [timestamp(row['observed_at']) for row in samples]
    if any(t < start or t >= end for t in times): blockers.add('sample_outside_window')
    if any(b < a for a, b in zip(times, times[1:])): blockers.add('sample_time_order')
    slots = [int((t - start).total_seconds() // 15) for t in times if start <= t < end]
    if len(set(slots)) != len(slots): blockers.add('duplicate_samples')
    coverage = len(set(slots)) / math.ceil(duration / 15)
    boundaries = [start, *times, end]
    if coverage < .95 or any((b-a).total_seconds() > 30 for a, b in zip(boundaries, boundaries[1:])):
        blockers.add('coverage_gap')
    revisions = {row['scope_revision'] for row in samples}
    if len(revisions) != 1 or not next(iter(revisions), ''):
        blockers.add('scope_changed_or_missing')
    counters = ('submitted_total', 'settled_total')
    metrics = (*counters, 'backlog_high', 'backlog_normal', 'oldest_normal_age_seconds')
    for row in samples:
        for key in metrics: number(row[key])
        expected, seen = row['workers_expected'], row['workers_seen']
        if type(expected) is not int or type(seen) is not int or expected < 1 or seen != expected:
            blockers.add('workers_missing')
        if row['dispatch_paused'] is not False: blockers.add('dispatch_paused')
    if len({row['workers_expected'] for row in samples}) != 1:
        blockers.add('workers_missing')
    for prior, current in zip(samples, samples[1:]):
        if any(current[key] < prior[key] for key in counters): blockers.add('counter_reset')
    # A long zero-return interval with backlog is not a successful quiet tail.
    for key, threshold, code in [('settled_total', 1800, 'no_returns_with_backlog'),
                                 ('submitted_total', 900, 'no_submissions_with_backlog')]:
        stalled_at = None
        for index, row in enumerate(samples):
            backlog = row['backlog_high'] + row['backlog_normal']
            if not backlog or index == 0 or row[key] != samples[index-1][key]:
                stalled_at = times[index] if backlog else None
            if stalled_at is not None and (times[index] - stalled_at).total_seconds() >= threshold:
                blockers.add(code)
    if samples:
        if samples[-1]['backlog_normal'] > samples[0]['backlog_normal']:
            blockers.add('normal_backlog_growing')
        if samples[-1]['oldest_normal_age_seconds'] > samples[0]['oldest_normal_age_seconds']:
            blockers.add('normal_backlog_aging')
        if sum(samples[-1][key] for key in ('backlog_high', 'backlog_normal')) > sum(
                samples[0][key] for key in ('backlog_high', 'backlog_normal')):
            blockers.add('total_backlog_growing')

    ids, classes, completed = set(), set(), {'high': 0, 'normal': 0}
    # Sustained mixed-load validation needs arrivals, not merely a drained batch.
    # One arrival per class per complete hour is only a minimum evidence gate;
    # it deliberately does not certify the contractual daily volume.
    arrival_hours = [{'high': 0, 'normal': 0} for _ in range(int(duration // 3600))]
    overdue, pending_high = [], 0
    for item in items:
        identity, service = item['event_id'], item['service_class']
        if not identity or identity in ids: blockers.add('item_inventory_invalid')
        ids.add(identity)
        if service not in completed: raise ValueError('invalid service class')
        classes.add(service)
        first = timestamp(item['first_queued_at'])
        result = timestamp(item['usable_result_at']) if item.get('usable_result_at') else None
        if first >= end or (result is not None and result < first): blockers.add('item_time_invalid')
        hour = int((first - start).total_seconds() // 3600)
        if 0 <= hour < len(arrival_hours):
            arrival_hours[hour][service] += 1
        # Results persisted AFTER this window cannot erase its unfinished age.
        usable = result is not None and result <= end
        if usable and result >= start: completed[service] += 1
        if service == 'high':
            if not usable: pending_high += 1
            if ((result if usable else end) - first).total_seconds() > 18000:
                overdue.append(identity)
    if classes != {'high', 'normal'}: blockers.add('mixed_workload_missing')
    if not completed['normal']: blockers.add('normal_progress_missing')
    if not completed['high']: blockers.add('high_progress_missing')
    if any(not counts['high'] or not counts['normal'] for counts in arrival_hours):
        blockers.add('continuous_input_missing')
    if overdue: blockers.add('high_sla_violation')

    event_ids = set()
    valid_four_hour = False
    for event in events:
        key, occurred = event['event_id'], timestamp(event['occurred_at'])
        if not key or key in event_ids: blockers.add('event_invalid')
        if event['kind'] in {'stop_condition', 'gap', 'sample_error'}:
            blockers.add('logged_'+event['kind'])
        event_ids.add(key)
        if event['kind'] == 'four_hour':
            if (occurred-start).total_seconds() < 14400 or occurred > end:
                blockers.add('four_hour_event_invalid')
            else: valid_four_hour = True
        digest = event.get('log_hash', '')
        if (type(event.get('log_sequence')) is not int or event['log_sequence'] < 1
                or not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in digest)
                or not event.get('logged_at') or timestamp(event['logged_at']) < occurred):
            blockers.add('event_not_persisted')
    if not valid_four_hour: blockers.add('four_hour_event_missing')
    if evidence_kind not in {'actual_e2e', 'synthetic'}:
        raise ValueError('evidence_kind must be actual_e2e or synthetic')
    software_passed = not blockers
    if evidence_kind != 'actual_e2e': blockers.add('synthetic_not_capacity_evidence')
    return {'schema_version': 'new-server-window-v1', 'accepted': not blockers,
            'software_window_passed': software_passed, 'blockers': sorted(blockers),
            'window_seconds': duration, 'coverage': coverage,
            'high_overdue_event_ids': overdue, 'pending_high': pending_high,
            'completed_in_window': completed, 'daily_100k_proven': False,
            'arrivals_by_complete_hour': arrival_hours,
            'limitations': ['source_inventory_and_log_chain_require_audit',
                           'pending_items_require_followup_until_terminal',
                           'four_hours_is_not_daily_sustainable_capacity']}

===== FILE backend/app/new_server_capacity.py =====
"""Candidate software envelopes, never proof of measured machine capacity."""
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CapacityEnvelope:
    processes: int
    slots_per_process: int
    phase_threads: int
    db_pool_size: int
    # 2026-09-16 实测线上 max_connections=512（历史峰值 53）；旧默认 200 把连接预算低估了 2.5 倍。
    mysql_max_connections: int = 512
    reserved_connections: int = 64

    def __post_init__(self):
        values = (self.processes, self.slots_per_process, self.phase_threads,
                  self.db_pool_size, self.mysql_max_connections, self.reserved_connections)
        if any(type(v) is not int or v < 1 for v in values):
            raise ValueError('capacity fields must be positive integers')
        if not (2 <= self.processes <= 8 and self.slots_per_process <= 256
                and self.total_slots <= 768 and self.phase_threads <= 8):
            raise ValueError('candidate exceeds bounded software envelope')
        if (self.db_pool_size < self.phase_threads + 2
                or self.connection_budget > self.mysql_max_connections):
            raise ValueError('database connection budget is insufficient')

    @property
    def total_slots(self):
        return self.processes * self.slots_per_process

    @property
    def connection_budget(self):
        # zero pool overflow; reserve covers web, auxiliaries and maintenance.
        return self.processes * self.db_pool_size + self.reserved_connections

    @property
    def validated(self):
        return False

    @classmethod
    def for_stage(cls, total):
        choices = {96: (3, 32, 1), 192: (3, 64, 4),
                   384: (6, 64, 4), 768: (6, 128, 8)}
        if type(total) is not int or total not in choices:
            raise ValueError('stage must be 96, 192, 384 or 768')
        processes, slots, threads = choices[total]
        return cls(processes, slots, threads, threads + 2)

    def as_dict(self):
        return {**asdict(self), 'total_slots': self.total_slots,
                'connection_budget': self.connection_budget,
                'validated': False, 'production_ready': False,
                'database_max_overflow': 0,
                'blockers': ['machine_specific_validation_required']}

===== FILE backend/app/node_correction_api.py =====
"""Append-only node correction API for v3 evaluation results."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, ValidationError, field_validator
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .category_evaluation_contract import (
    DeductionRuleHit,
    DimensionDeductionOutput,
    NodeCorrection,
    NodeCorrectionEvidence,
)
from .database import get_db
from .evaluation_v3_pipeline import V3PipelineError, recompute_qualified_v3
from .models import EvaluationResult
from .schema_adapter import validate_production_correction
from .worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring


CALL_A_FIELDS = {
    "score",
    "grade",
    "title",
    "seotitle",
    "category",
    "style",
    "tags",
    "cons",
    "design",
    "reason",
    "image_defects",
    "trait",
}


class CorrectNodeRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    correction_key: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=120)
    node_type: str
    node_path: str
    old_value: Any
    new_value: Any
    evidence: list[NodeCorrectionEvidence] = Field(default_factory=list)
    reason: str
    # 结构化归因码，供纠偏分析聚合；自由文本的 reason 无法统计。
    reason_codes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)

    @field_validator("node_type")
    @classmethod
    def _node_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
        allowed = {
            "call_a_field",
            "precheck_field",
            "redline",
            "track",
            "dimension_rule",
            "aesthetic_score",
            "final_level",
        }
        if value not in allowed:
            raise ValueError(f"node_type 必须是 {sorted(allowed)} 之一")
        return value

    @field_validator("node_path", "reason")
    @classmethod
    def _not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        value = value.strip()
        if not value:
            raise ValueError("字段不能为空")
        return value


def _coded(status: int, code: str, message: str) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(status_code=status, detail={"code": code, "message": message})


def _json_value(value: str | None, fallback: Any) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return fallback


def _path_parts(path: str, *, prefix: str) -> list[str]:
    raw = path[len(prefix) :] if path.startswith(prefix) else path
    parts = [part for part in raw.split(".") if part]
    if not parts or any(not part.replace("_", "").isalnum() for part in parts):
        raise _coded(400, "node_path_invalid", f"非法节点路径：{path}")
    return parts


def _get_dict_path(root: dict[str, Any], parts: list[str]) -> Any:
    current: Any = root
    for part in parts:
        if not isinstance(current, dict) or part not in current:
            raise _coded(400, "node_path_not_found", f"节点路径不存在：{'.'.join(parts)}")
        current = current[part]
    return current


def _set_dict_path(root: dict[str, Any], parts: list[str], value: Any) -> None:
    current: Any = root
    for part in parts[:-1]:
        if not isinstance(current, dict) or part not in current:
            raise _coded(400, "node_path_not_found", f"节点路径不存在：{'.'.join(parts)}")
        current = current[part]
    if not isinstance(current, dict) or parts[-1] not in current:
        raise _coded(400, "node_path_not_found", f"节点路径不存在：{'.'.join(parts)}")
    current[parts[-1]] = value


def _grade_for_score(score: int) -> str:
    if score >= 81:
        return "L1"
    if score >= 61:
        return "L2"
    if score >= 41:
        return "L3"
    if score >= 21:
        return "L4"
    return "L5"


def _call_a_field(path: str) -> str:
    parts = _path_parts(path, prefix="call_a.")
    from .model_3d_su_legacy_projection import RISK_FIELD_PHRASES

    risk_fields = {f"risk_{key}" for key in RISK_FIELD_PHRASES}
    if not path.startswith("call_a.") or len(parts) != 1 or parts[0] not in CALL_A_FIELDS | risk_fields:
        raise _coded(
            400,
            "node_path_invalid",
            "调用A字段路径必须是 call_a.<字段名>",
        )
    return parts[0]


def _dimension_definition(
    config: Any, dimension_key: str
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not isinstance(config, dict):
        return None
    for group_name in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
        group = config.get(group_name)
        if not isinstance(group, dict):
            continue
        schema = group.get("schema_definition")
        dimensions = schema.get("dimensions") if isinstance(schema, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(dimensions, list):
            dimensions = group.get("dimensions")
        if not isinstance(dimensions, list):
            continue
        for dimension in dimensions:
            if isinstance(dimension, dict) and dimension.get("key") == dimension_key:
                return dimension
    return None


def _configured_dimension_rule_ids(
    config: Any, *, dimension_key: str, hit_field: str
) -> set[str]:
    dimension = _dimension_definition(config, dimension_key)
    rule_field = "bonus_rules" if hit_field == "hit_bonus_rules" else "deduction_rules"
    raw_rules = dimension.get(rule_field) if isinstance(dimension, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(raw_rules, list):
        return set()
    return {
        str(rule["rule_id"])
        for rule in raw_rules
        if isinstance(rule, dict) and isinstance(rule.get("rule_id"), str)
    }


def _dimension_node(
    output: dict[str, Any], path: str, *, frozen_config: Any = None
) -> tuple[Any, Callable[[Any], None]]:
    parts = _path_parts(path, prefix="dimension.")
    if len(parts) not in {2, 3} or parts[1] not in {
        "hit_rules",
        "hit_bonus_rules",
    }:
        raise _coded(
            400,
            "node_path_invalid",
            "维度路径应为 dimension.<维度key>.hit_rules[.<rule_id>] 或 "
            "dimension.<维度key>.hit_bonus_rules[.<rule_id>]",
        )
    dimension_key = parts[0]
    hit_field = parts[1]
    dimensions = output.get("dimensions")
    if isinstance(dimensions, dict):
        target = dimensions.get(dimension_key)
    else:
        # Results written by the first bridge-v1 deployment used an array.
        target = next(
            (
                item
                for item in dimensions or []
                if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("dimension_key") == dimension_key
            ),
            None,
        )
    if target is None:
        raise _coded(400, "dimension_not_found", f"未找到维度 {dimension_key}")
    hits = target.setdefault(hit_field, [])
    if not isinstance(hits, list):
        raise _coded(400, "dimension_rule_invalid", "规则命中必须是数组")
    configured_rule_ids = _configured_dimension_rule_ids(
        frozen_config,
        dimension_key=dimension_key,
        hit_field=hit_field,
    )

    def validate_configured(rule_id: str) -> None:
        if rule_id not in configured_rule_ids:
            polarity = "加分" if hit_field == "hit_bonus_rules" else "扣分"
            raise _coded(
                400,
                "rule_unknown",
                f"维度 {dimension_key} 未配置{polarity}规则 {rule_id}",
            )

    if len(parts) == 2:
        def assign_all(value: Any) -> None:
            try:
                parsed = DimensionDeductionOutput.model_validate(
                    {
                        "dimension_key": dimension_key,
                        hit_field: value,
                    }
                )
            except ValidationError as exc:
                raise _coded(
                    400,
                    "dimension_rule_invalid",
                    "规则命中必须使用 high/medium/low 置信度枚举",
                ) from exc
            parsed_hits = getattr(parsed, hit_field)
            for item in parsed_hits:
                validate_configured(item.rule_id)
            target[hit_field] = [
                item.model_dump(mode="json") for item in parsed_hits
            ]

        return hits, assign_all
    rule_id = parts[2]
    validate_configured(rule_id)
    index = next(
        (i for i, hit in enumerate(hits) if hit.get("rule_id") == rule_id), None
    )
    old = hits[index] if index is not None else None

    def assign(value: Any) -> None:
        if value is None:
            if index is not None:
                hits.pop(index)
            return
        try:
            parsed = DeductionRuleHit.model_validate(value)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise _coded(
                400,
                "dimension_rule_invalid",
                "新规则命中必须使用 high/medium/low 置信度枚举",
            ) from exc
        if parsed.rule_id != rule_id:
            raise _coded(400, "dimension_rule_invalid", "新规则命中必须是同 rule_id 的对象")
        value = parsed.model_dump(mode="json")
        if index is None:
            hits.append(value)
        else:
            hits[index] = value

    return old, assign


def _response(result: EvaluationResult, correction: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "evaluation_result_id": result.id,
        "score": result.score,
        "level": result.level,
        "confidence": result.confidence,
        "needs_review": result.needs_review,
        "precheck": _json_value(result.precheck_json, {}),
        "aesthetic": _json_value(result.aesthetic_json, None),
        "scoring": _json_value(result.scoring_json, {}),
        "correction": correction,
        "correction_history": _json_value(result.correction_history_json, []),
    }


def apply_node_correction(
    db: Session,
    *,
    result: EvaluationResult,
    payload: CorrectNodeRequest,
    corrector: str,
    corrector_confidence: float | None = None,
    corrector_policy: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Apply one correction through the same append-only v3 replay path.

    The caller owns the surrounding transaction.  Both the authenticated API
    and the automatic corrector use this function so automatic changes remain
    visible in the existing node-correction editor and can be overridden by a
    later human event.
    """

    scoring = _json_value(result.scoring_json, {})
    if not isinstance(scoring, dict):
        scoring = {}
    history = _json_value(result.correction_history_json, [])
    if payload.correction_key:
        previous = next(
            (
                item
                for item in history
                if item.get("correction_key") == payload.correction_key
            ),
            None,
        )
        if previous is not None:
            return _response(result, previous)

    precheck = _json_value(result.precheck_json, {})
    if not isinstance(precheck, dict):
        precheck = {}

    if payload.node_type == "call_a_field":
        field = _call_a_field(payload.node_path)
        production_fields = precheck.get("production_fields")
        if not isinstance(production_fields, dict):
            production_fields = {}
        risk_fields = precheck.get("model_3d_su_fields")
        if not isinstance(risk_fields, dict):
            risk_fields = {}

        if field == "score":
            old_value = result.score
        elif field == "grade":
            old_value = result.level
        elif field.startswith("risk_"):
            risk_key = field[len("risk_"):]
            if risk_key not in risk_fields:
                raise _coded(
                    409,
                    "call_a_field_missing",
                    f"旧评测未存储调用A风险字段 {risk_key}，请重跑后再纠偏",
                )
            old_value = risk_fields[risk_key]
        else:
            if field not in production_fields:
                raise _coded(
                    409,
                    "call_a_field_missing",
                    f"旧评测未存储调用A字段 {field}，请重跑后再纠偏",
                )
            old_value = production_fields[field]

        if old_value != payload.old_value:
            raise _coded(409, "node_value_conflict", "节点当前值已变化，请刷新后重试")

        downstream_recomputed = False
        if field == "score":
            try:
                validate_production_correction(
                    "production_fields.score", payload.new_value
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise _coded(400, "call_a_field_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
            new_score = int(payload.new_value)
            new_grade = _grade_for_score(new_score)
            result.score = new_score
            result.level = new_grade
            result.needs_review = False
            scoring["score"] = new_score
            scoring["level"] = new_grade
            scoring["manual_call_a_score"] = new_score
            scoring.pop("manual_call_a_grade", None)
            result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
            if "score" in production_fields:
                production_fields["score"] = new_score
            downstream_recomputed = True
        elif field == "grade":
            if payload.new_value not in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}:
                raise _coded(400, "level_invalid", "新等级必须是 L1-L5")
            result.level = payload.new_value
            result.needs_review = False
            scoring["level"] = payload.new_value
            scoring["manual_call_a_grade"] = payload.new_value
            result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
        elif field.startswith("risk_"):
            risk_key = field[len("risk_"):]
            try:
                validate_production_correction(
                    f"model_3d_su_fields.{risk_key}", payload.new_value
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise _coded(400, "call_a_field_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
            risk_fields[risk_key] = payload.new_value
            precheck["model_3d_su_fields"] = risk_fields
            result.precheck_json = json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False)
        else:
            try:
                validate_production_correction(
                    f"production_fields.{field}", payload.new_value
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise _coded(400, "call_a_field_invalid", str(exc)) from exc
            production_fields[field] = payload.new_value

        if production_fields and not field.startswith("risk_"):
            precheck["production_fields"] = production_fields
            result.precheck_json = json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False)

        correction = NodeCorrection(
            correction_key=payload.correction_key,
            node_type=payload.node_type,
            node_path=payload.node_path,
            old_value=old_value,
            new_value=payload.new_value,
            evidence=payload.evidence,
            reason=payload.reason,
            reason_codes=payload.reason_codes,
            corrector=corrector,
            corrector_confidence=corrector_confidence,
            corrector_policy=corrector_policy,
            corrected_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            downstream_recomputed=downstream_recomputed,
        ).model_dump(mode="json")
        history.append(correction)
        result.correction_history_json = json.dumps(history, ensure_ascii=False)
        db.flush()
        return _response(result, correction)

    context = scoring.get("v3_context")
    if not isinstance(context, dict):
        raise _coded(409, "not_v3_rule_result", "该结果不含可重放的 v3 冻结上下文")
    dimension_output = _json_value(result.aesthetic_json, {})
    previous_scoring = dict(scoring)
    track_override: str | None = None
    downstream_recomputed = payload.node_type != "final_level"

    if payload.node_type in {"precheck_field", "redline"}:
        prefix = "redline." if payload.node_path.startswith("redline.") else "precheck."
        parts = _path_parts(payload.node_path, prefix=prefix)
        old_value = _get_dict_path(precheck, parts)
        assign: Callable[[Any], None] = lambda value: _set_dict_path(
            precheck, parts, value
        )
    elif payload.node_type == "dimension_rule":
        if not isinstance(dimension_output, dict):
            raise _coded(409, "dimension_output_missing", "结果缺少维度规则命中输出")
        dimensions_by_track = context.get("subcategory_dimensions")
        frozen_config = (
            dimensions_by_track.get(scoring.get("track_key"))
            if isinstance(dimensions_by_track, dict)
            else None
        )
        old_value, assign = _dimension_node(
            dimension_output,
            payload.node_path,
            frozen_config=frozen_config,
        )
    elif payload.node_type == "aesthetic_score":
        if not isinstance(dimension_output, dict):
            raise _coded(
                409, "dimension_output_missing", "结果缺少调用B输出，无法纠偏美感分"
            )
        if payload.node_path not in {
            "aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
            "aesthetic_score",
            "call_b.aesthetic_score",
        }:
            raise _coded(
                400, "node_path_invalid", "美感分路径必须是 aesthetic.aesthetic_score"
            )
        old_value = dimension_output.get("aesthetic_score")
        if (
            isinstance(payload.new_value, bool)
            or not isinstance(payload.new_value, int)
            or not 0 <= payload.new_value <= 100
        ):
            raise _coded(400, "aesthetic_score_invalid", "美感分必须是 0-100 的整数")

        def _assign_aesthetic_score(value: Any) -> None:
            dimension_output["aesthetic_score"] = value
            # 美感基座校验要求非空可见证据，否则重放会 fail-closed 直接失败。
            # 人工填写的纠偏理由就是这个新分数的证据，同时保留模型原有证据供对照。
            existing = dimension_output.get("aesthetic_evidence")
            texts = [
                item.strip()
                for item in (existing if isinstance(existing, list) else [])
                if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
            ]
            human_note = payload.reason.strip()
            if human_note and human_note not in texts:
                texts.insert(0, human_note)
            dimension_output["aesthetic_evidence"] = texts or [
                f"人工判定美感分为 {value}"
            ]
            # 人工真值不再沿用模型置信度，否则模型的低置信会被误读成人工不确定。
            dimension_output["aesthetic_confidence"] = 1.0
            dimension_output["manual_aesthetic_score"] = True

        assign = _assign_aesthetic_score
    elif payload.node_type == "track":
        old_value = scoring.get("track_key")
        if payload.node_path not in {"track", "track_key", "scoring.track_key"}:
            raise _coded(400, "node_path_invalid", "赛道节点路径必须是 track_key")
        if not isinstance(payload.new_value, str):
            raise _coded(400, "track_invalid", "新赛道必须是字符串")
        track_override = payload.new_value
        assign = lambda _value: None
    else:
        old_value = result.level
        if payload.node_path not in {"final_level", "level", "scoring.level"}:
            raise _coded(400, "node_path_invalid", "最终等级路径必须是 final_level")
        if payload.new_value not in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}:
            raise _coded(400, "level_invalid", "新等级必须是 L1-L5")
        assign = lambda _value: None

    if old_value != payload.old_value:
        raise _coded(409, "node_value_conflict", "节点当前值已变化，请刷新后重试")
    assign(payload.new_value)

    if downstream_recomputed:
        try:
            replayed = recompute_qualified_v3(
                v3_context=context,
                precheck=precheck,
                dimension_output=dimension_output,
                track_key=track_override,
            )
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, V3PipelineError) as exc:
            raise _coded(
                400,
                getattr(exc, "code", "node_recompute_failed"),
                f"节点纠偏无法重算：{exc}",
            ) from exc
        scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(replayed, precheck=precheck)
        scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
        scoring["v3_context"] = context
        scoring["v3_config_revision"] = context.get("config_revision")
        scoring["dimension_mode"] = previous_scoring.get("dimension_mode", "all")
        scoring["dimension_selection"] = previous_scoring.get("dimension_selection")
        manual_score = previous_scoring.get("manual_call_a_score")
        if (
            isinstance(manual_score, int)
            and not isinstance(manual_score, bool)
            and 0 <= manual_score <= 100
        ):
            scoring["manual_call_a_score"] = manual_score
            scoring["score"] = manual_score
            scoring["level"] = _grade_for_score(manual_score)
        manual_grade = previous_scoring.get("manual_call_a_grade")
        if manual_grade in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}:
            scoring["manual_call_a_grade"] = manual_grade
            scoring["level"] = manual_grade
        if isinstance(replayed.get("dimension_deduction_output"), dict):
            dimension_output = replayed["dimension_deduction_output"]
        result.precheck_json = json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.aesthetic_json = json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.score = scoring.get("score")
        result.level = scoring.get("level")
        result.confidence = scoring.get("confidence")
        result.needs_review = bool(scoring.get("needs_review"))
    else:
        scoring["level"] = payload.new_value
        scoring["manual_final_level"] = payload.new_value
        scoring["manual_call_a_grade"] = payload.new_value
        result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.level = payload.new_value
        result.needs_review = False

    correction = NodeCorrection(
        correction_key=payload.correction_key,
        node_type=payload.node_type,
        node_path=payload.node_path,
        old_value=old_value,
        new_value=payload.new_value,
        evidence=payload.evidence,
        reason=payload.reason,
        reason_codes=payload.reason_codes,
        corrector=corrector,
        corrector_confidence=corrector_confidence,
        corrector_policy=corrector_policy,
        corrected_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        downstream_recomputed=downstream_recomputed,
    ).model_dump(mode="json")
    history.append(correction)
    result.correction_history_json = json.dumps(history, ensure_ascii=False)
    db.flush()
    return _response(result, correction)


def build_node_correction_router(require_reviewer: Callable[..., Any]) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(tags=["evaluation-node-correction"])

    @router.post("/api/evaluation-results/{evaluation_id}/correct-node")
    def correct_node(
        evaluation_id: int,
        payload: CorrectNodeRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        result = db.get(EvaluationResult, evaluation_id)
        if result is None:
            raise _coded(404, "evaluation_result_not_found", "评测结果不存在")
        response = apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=result,
            payload=payload,
            corrector=str(
                getattr(user, "display_name", None)
                or getattr(user, "username", "unknown")
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(result)
        return response

    return router
